*Ahmad Hazimeh,Adrian Herrera,Srividya Subramanian,Thaqiya Aman,Sara Vaccino,Qiang Liu,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍开源、具备真实漏洞标注的模糊测试基准 Magma，并概述其在统一评估和比较不同模糊测试器方面的作用及后续扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同模糊测试器常采用不一致的目标程序、漏洞判定标准和实验配置，导致评估结果难以公平比较。Magma 旨在通过统一且具有真实漏洞依据的基准，提升实验的可复现性、可比性与可信度。

Method: 论文说明 Magma 的设计理念、基准构成和使用方式：以真实软件中的漏洞为测试目标，为漏洞触发与检测提供明确依据，并以统一实验框架支持多种模糊测试器的评测。本文还描述了相对于早期版本新增的扩展。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据或量化结论，但指出 Magma 已形成具有影响力的评估基础设施，可用于对模糊测试器进行统一比较；扩展进一步增强了该基准的覆盖范围或实用性。

Conclusion: Magma 的核心价值在于为模糊测试研究提供开放、标准化且基于真实漏洞的评估平台，从而减少评测偏差并促进结果复现。由于摘要未详述扩展内容和实验结果，其具体技术改进与效果仍需查阅正文。

Abstract: Magma is an open-source and ground-truth fuzzing benchmark that enables uniform fuzzer evaluation and comparison. Magma was originally released with a research paper published at ACM SIGMETRICS 2021. This short paper explains the motivation, the design, and the impact of Magma, with a description of extensions to the original benchmark.

</details>


### [25] [When Verified Source Becomes Attack Input: Defending Smart Contracts Against LLM-Based Vulnerability Scanning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28400)
*Mingyuan Huang,Zimo Ji,Yifan Mo,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出DeLLMGuard，一种通过多地址部署、代理/委托/工厂关系拆分及验证层机制，降低基于大语言模型的自动化合约漏洞分析效果、同时保留源码公开与授权审计能力的部署框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开验证源码虽然增强透明度与用户信任，却也降低了自动化漏洞分析的门槛。作者认为，现有部署方式使智能合约的可读源码与实际运行逻辑直接对应，因而需要一种兼顾公开披露、业务等价性验证和抗自动化分析能力的机制。

Method: DeLLMGuard将对外披露的源码与运行时执行逻辑分布到多个合约地址，使分析系统在进行漏洞判断前必须额外恢复代理、委托调用和工厂部署等跨合约关系。框架内置Verification Layer，用于核验部署关系、运行时代码、源码及状态变化，以证明转换后的部署仍保持原始业务实现。实验基于387个真实存在缺陷的合约，并在由SCONE-bench派生的环境中测试三个大语言模型代理；同时进行执行轨迹分析和消融实验。

Result: 在实验设置下，DeLLMGuard将代理对漏洞根因的总体判断正确率从23.5%降至6.6%。在主要的非代理合约集合上，其效果优于仅隐藏源码、直接面对字节码的闭源基线。轨迹与消融结果表明，代理虽然常能恢复部分下游合约，但仍难以定位真实缺陷，说明跨合约关系恢复与语义推理是自动化分析的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: DeLLMGuard表明，可通过保持可验证业务等价性的部署重构，在不完全取消源码披露和授权审计的前提下，提高自动化大模型合约分析的难度。论文的核心贡献在于将防护重点从单纯隐藏代码转向增加跨合约执行关系的恢复成本；但其实际安全性仍依赖于验证层的完备性、部署模式的适用范围，以及面对更强程序分析工具时的长期鲁棒性。

Abstract: Smart contracts are financial programs deployed on blockchains to manage digital assets. To build trust with users and investors, smart contract projects typically publish their source code on blockchain explorers and verify it against the deployed bytecode, making the on-chain program accessible through a human-readable implementation. However, LLM agents are changing the threat model of this disclosure mechanism. By leveraging publicly disclosed source code, recent agent workflows make it increasingly practical to scan contract vulnerabilities for exploits at large scale.
  In this paper, we propose DeLLMGuard, a smart contract deployment framework that defends against malicious LLM-based vulnerability scanning while preserving public source disclosure and authorized auditing. DeLLMGuard can separate disclosed source code from runtime execution through multiple contract addresses in a real-world blockchain environment. LLM agents must therefore recover additional proxy, delegate, and factory relations before vulnerability analysis. A built-in Verification Layer checks deployment relations, runtime bytecode, source code, and state changes to ensure that the transformation preserves the original business implementation. We evaluate DeLLMGuard on 387 real-world vulnerable contracts with three LLM agents in an environment derived from SCONE-bench. DeLLMGuard reduces overall root-cause correctness from 23.5% to 6.6% and outperforms the closed-source bytecode baseline on the primary non-proxy set. Trace and ablation analyses further show that agents often recover downstream contracts but still fail to identify the vulnerability, indicating that cross-contract recovery remains a major challenge for automated LLM scanning.

</details>


### [26] [TagZilla: Automated Owner and Abuse Type Tagging for Indicators of Compromise in Threat Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28124)
*Gibran Gomez,Juan Caballero*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TagZilla平台，用于从非结构化网络威胁情报报告中自动提取并为失陷指标（IoC）添加归属实体和滥用类型等上下文标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有报告中的IP地址、URL、文件哈希和加密货币钱包等IoC通常缺少与威胁组织、恶意软件家族及滥用行为相关的上下文，降低了其在研判、关联分析和防御中的可用性。

Method: TagZilla对报告文本进行自动分析：采用基于大语言模型的开放世界分类为IoC分配归属标签，识别其关联的威胁组织、恶意软件家族或其他实体；同时采用封闭世界分类，为IoC分配29类预定义滥用类型标签。系统可处理同一报告涉及多个实体和多个恶意软件家族的情况。

Result: 在包含100份报告和1,534个指标的人工标注测试集上，归属标签任务的F1值为0.94，滥用类型标签任务的F1值为0.93。将系统应用于765份报告后，识别并标注了15,583个IoC，覆盖637个恶意软件家族、113个威胁组织及162个其他实体。

Conclusion: TagZilla能够以较高准确度为报告中的IoC补充可操作的上下文信息，并在多实体、多恶意软件场景下保持有效，从而支持构建面向实体的IoC画像并提升威胁情报的关联与利用价值。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) reports often describe Indicators of Compromise (IoCs) such as IP addresses, URLs, file hashes, and cryptocurrency wallets involved in cyberattacks. Those IoCs are typically described in the unstructured report's text, or listed at the end of the report with little context, limiting their usefulness. This paper presents TagZilla, a platform that, given a threat report, automatically analyzes its text and tags the IoCs it describes with contextual information about the threat group and malware family that the IoC belongs to and the type of abuse associated with the IoC (e.g., phishing, sextortion, command-and-control). TagZilla provides a novel LLM-based approach to assign owner tags to IoCs using an open-world classification, and assigns 29 abuse type tags to IoCs using a closed-world classification. We evaluate TagZilla on a manually generated ground truth of 100 threat reports containing 1,534 indicators, where it achieves an F1 score of 0.94 for owner tagging and 0.93 for abuse type tagging. Then, we apply TagZilla to tag 765 threat reports, identifying 15,583 IoCs belonging to 637 malware families, 113 threat groups, and 162 other entities. The results show that TagZilla can tag IoCs even in reports describing multiple actors and malware families, enabling the generation of IoC profiles for those entities.

</details>


### [27] [Layered LLM Defenses as an Ensemble: Access Tiers, Inference Cost, and the Measured Failure Correlation Between Defense Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28327)
*Abrar Alotaibi,Muhammad Shahid Jabbar,Sadam Al-Azani,Moataz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出，LLM安全防御栈的效果不能仅按各层独立防御能力相乘来估计；实测显示各层失败高度正相关，使多层叠加的真实收益有限，却显著增加误拒率与推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践常假定叠加多种防御会形成复合增益，但这一假设依赖“不同防御在不同输入上失败”的独立性条件。论文认为该条件虽被安全研究普遍建议，却缺乏直接测量，因此需要建立统一的访问权限、成本与相关性分析框架。

Method: 论文提出对手访问层级模型（AATM），将对手能力由仅系统访问的A0分级至可影响训练数据的A4；同时按推理期开销将防御划分为五类，并依据是否需要权重或激活读取来界定防御者权限。作者在七层防御栈上运行自适应对手，计算防御层失败事件的φ相关系数、联合残余攻击成功率与独立性乘法预测的偏差，并通过按行为难度分层、置换检验、多数投票标注和外部校准阈值进行稳健性验证。

Result: 十五个可测防御层配对的失败相关性均为正，φ介于0.30至0.75。实际联合残余攻击成功率最高比独立性乘法预测高0.172。按行为难度分层后，大部分相关性消失，表明依赖主要来自共同原因；但统计检验仍支持残余相关性存在。该七层栈对良性提示的误拒率达到约80%，而整体安全表现与其中最强的单层防御在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 防御栈的覆盖率会在同一权限层级内趋于饱和，成本随防御类别累加，误拒率按并集累积；只有失败独立时，残余攻击成功率才会乘法下降。由于多个防御通常包裹同一基础模型，其相关性具有架构性，扩大候选防御池无法自然消除。因此，防御多样性只能辅助成员选择，不能预测最终栈效果；实际部署应进行端到端测量，并同时评估安全增益、推理成本和误拒代价。

Abstract: Practitioners defend large language models (LLMs) by stacking defenses, assuming the layers compound. A stack is an ensemble, and ensembles compound only under a condition the LLM security literature recommends but never measures: the members must fail on different inputs.
  Two instruments make that measurable. The Adversary Access-Tier Model (AATM) grades an adversary by the access it holds, from system-only (A0) to influence over training data (A4). A cost model sorts defenses into five classes of inference-time overhead; because two classes require training weights or reading activations, they tier the defender as AATM tiers the adversary. From these we derive how a stack behaves, and the quantities a defender cares about diverge: coverage saturates within a tier, cost rises by class, false refusals accumulate as a union, and residual attack success falls multiplicatively only under independence.
  We measure that independence. Running one adaptive adversary against a seven-layer stack, failure correlation is positive in all fifteen measurable pairs ($φ$ from $0.30$ to $0.75$), and the joint residual exceeds the multiplicative prediction by up to $0.172$. Stratifying on behavior difficulty dissolves most of the association, so the dependence is predominantly common-cause, but it survives permutation inference, majority-vote grader labels, and externally calibrated thresholds. The same stack refuses four in five benign prompts while remaining statistically indistinguishable from its strongest single layer.
  The dependence is architectural rather than sampling-based: members correlate through the model they all wrap, so no wider member pool weakens it. Diversity therefore selects stack members but does not predict what an assembled stack delivers, which has to be measured end to end.

</details>


### [28] [False-CSI Attacks in Power-Domain NOMA for 6G: A Threat Taxonomy and System-Level Impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28351)
*Samira Jafarli,Aysha Ebrahim,Suleyman Uludag*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理功率域NOMA中的虚假信道状态信息（CSI）问题，指出其会篡改基站控制决策，并影响功率分配、串行干扰消除（SIC）、调度、公平性、吞吐量与保密性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功率域NOMA依赖CSI完成用户排序、配对、聚类、波束成形及功率分配。若CSI被主动伪造而非仅受随机噪声影响，网络控制环路可能产生系统性偏差，因此需要将其作为控制输入完整性问题加以研究。

Method: 文章构建二维威胁分类法：按伪造幅度区分低报与高报，按对用户排序的影响区分保序、边界型和逆序型。随后扩展讨论协同虚假CSI、分组变更、方向伪造、导频欺骗、训练阶段注入及RIS诱导的信道操控，并将各类问题映射至系统级后果。

Result: 该分类表明，虚假CSI可通过改变信道强弱估计及用户相对次序，导致错误功率配置、SIC失败概率上升、调度偏置、用户公平性恶化、网络吞吐损失及保密能力下降。协同行为和可重构智能表面相关操控进一步扩大了影响范围。

Conclusion: 虚假CSI不应仅被视为信道估计误差，而应被定位为面向6G NOMA控制链路的输入完整性风险。后续防护应同时覆盖CSI真实性验证、训练与导频保护、排序和分组稳健性，以及功率控制与调度的异常检测。

Abstract: Power-domain non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) remains a widely studied technique for improving spectral efficiency and supporting dense connectivity in beyond-5G and 6G networks. Its main operating mechanisms, however, depend on the integrity of channel-state information (CSI). Power allocation, user ordering, pairing, clustering, and beamforming can all be distorted when the CSI consumed by the base station is deliberately biased rather than merely noisy. This article examines false CSI as an attack surface in power-domain NOMA. We organize the threat space using a compact taxonomy with two primary axes: magnitude, which distinguishes underreporting from overreporting, and ordering effect, which distinguishes order-preserving, boundary, and order-reversing attacks. We then show how coordinated false- CSI behavior, group-changing attacks, direction forgery, pilot spoofing, training-phase injection, and RIS-induced channel manipulation extend this basic taxonomy. Finally, we map each attack family to system-level impacts on power allocation, SIC reliability, scheduler behavior, fairness, throughput, and secrecy. The central message is that false CSI should be treated not only as a channel-estimation problem, but also as a control-input integrity problem for 6G NOMA.

</details>


### [29] [Optimal Adversarial Testing: Extracting Honest Test Results from Dishonest Test Takers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28362)
*Owen Cox,April Xu,Weiyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究存在自然噪声和作弊污染时的鲁棒测试策略，旨在通过最优重测恢复可靠的测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试结果可能因测量噪声及被测试者的对抗性行为而失真，尤其是人工智能辅助作弊使结果污染更加严重，因此需要能够抵御作弊的测试机制。

Method: 提出针对选定被测试群体的最优重测策略，并结合不同等级或类型的测试安全措施。通过动态规划方法对重测对象、测试顺序及安全措施进行优化。

Result: 理论上构建了能够在存在作弊者污染的情况下恢复所需测试结果的策略框架，并确定了相应的最优测试方案。摘要未报告具体实验数据、性能提升幅度或基准比较结果。

Conclusion: 动态规划可用于设计兼顾测试安全性与重测成本的最优测试策略，从而提高受噪声和作弊影响的测试结果的可靠性。

Abstract: In applications, it is often required to test objects or people to determine their qualities in terms of certain metrics. However, besides being naturally noisy, the test results can be corrupted by adversarial behaviors of objects or people being tested (test takers). For example, dishonest test takers can cheat in the exams to distort the test results. With the development of AI technologies, such distortions driven by cheating using AI technologies are becoming more commonplace and severe. In this paper, we propose optimal testing strategies which can still recover needed test results even if there are cheaters polluting the results. The proposed testing strategies will optimally re-test selected group of test takers using different testing security measures. We determine the optimal testing strategies using a dynamic programming method.

</details>


### [30] [CamoDocs: A Poisoning Attack Against Retrieval-Augmented Language Models Using Camouflaged Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28389)
*Jaewon Jung,Haizhong Zheng,Hongsun Jang,Jaeyong Song,Beidi Chen,Jinho Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) augments LLMs with external documents, but public or user-editable sources expose RAG systems to data poisoning: attackers can inject malicious documents to steer outputs toward targeted answers. Existing poisoning attacks often rely on query inclusion, inserting the target query into poisoned documents to improve retrieval; however, this creates lexical and embedding-space artifacts that make them easy to filter. We propose CamoDocs, a poisoning attack that avoids direct query inclusion by camouflaging adversarial documents among benign content. CamoDocs chunks synthesized benign and adversarial drafts, replaces selected tokens in benign chunks with dispersion tokens that spread poisoned-document embeddings, and applies coherence filtering to limit readability degradation. Across seven RAG defenses, three open-weight LLMs, and three benchmarks, CamoDocs achieves strong average ASR while avoiding query-overlap artifacts exploited by simple query detection. It also remains effective against proprietary models, achieving average ASRs of 61.80% on GPT-5.4-mini and 55.09% on Claude-Haiku-4.5. Finally, we show that erasure-heavy clustering defenses such as TrustRAG can reduce ASR, but only with substantial utility drops on retrieval-dependent benchmarks such as NeoQA. Code is available at https://github.com/jaewonalive/CamoDocs.

</details>


### [31] [BEACON: Behavior-Anchored Cross-Source Knowledge Graph Construction for Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28394)
*Changze Li,Yutong Cheng,Tsania Camila Finnisa,Qian Cui,Wei Ding,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出BEACON：一个由大语言模型驱动的跨来源网络威胁情报知识图谱构建框架。其核心是将报告中的攻击行为映射至MITRE ATT&CK技术，作为统一锚点，再据此连接上下文实体与失陷指标，并完成跨报告实体对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络威胁情报报告数量庞大、格式异构且主要为非结构化文本，人工构建知识图谱成本高。既有方法通常只抽取单篇报告中的局部信息，难以解决不同来源对同一威胁使用不同名称、实体难以归并的问题；同时，缺少同时支持“实体—攻击技术关联”和跨来源对齐标注的数据集与评测基准。

Method: BEACON包含两阶段。第一阶段以“提出—验证”范式将单篇报告抽取为图：模型先生成攻击行为、上下文实体和失陷指标等候选，再利用报告原文证据及官方ATT&CK技术定义进行验证，以降低技术误分类和生成幻觉。攻击行为被标准化映射为ATT&CK技术锚点，其他实体作为行为参与者或痕迹连接至锚点。第二阶段采用分层对齐策略融合各报告图，按确定性由强到弱依次使用字符级相似度、语义相似度和攻击技术邻域重叠等信号；图合并后邻域信息扩大，策略迭代执行以增强后续对齐。作者还从34个来源构建两个人工标注集：报告级抽取集含8,395个元素，跨来源整合集含3,487个标注。

Result: 在报告级CTI信息抽取和跨来源实体整合两个任务上，BEACON均优于全部对比方法；相对于最佳基线，性能提升分别至少为23%和9%。数据集规模方面，作者声称其报告级抽取集是目前最大的同类人工标注集，而跨来源整合集首次提供了相应的对齐真值。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，以标准化ATT&CK技术作为行为锚点，能够为异构CTI报告提供共享语义空间，并有效支持跨来源知识图谱构建。提出—验证式抽取提升了大语言模型输出的可证据化与可靠性，基于多层信号和动态邻域的实体对齐则缓解了跨报告别名与命名不一致问题。其贡献包括框架、跨来源对齐方法及新标注数据集；但摘要未说明对低质量文本、ATT&CK覆盖不足行为、时间演化实体及超大规模增量更新场景的适应性，仍需通过完整实验进一步评估。

Abstract: Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) is foundational to modern cyber defense, yet much of it resides in unstructured reports whose volume and heterogeneity far exceed manual analysis, motivating research on automatically constructing knowledge graphs from CTI reports. However, existing approaches mainly extract partial information within a single report, leaving the cross-source setting unexplored, where the same threat is given unrelated names. Our key insight is that attack behaviors, once mapped to MITRE ATT&CK (a standardized catalog of attack techniques), can anchor the rest of a report. Attack behaviors are the adversarial actions a report describes, while contextual entities (e.g., threat actors, campaigns, and affected products) and Indicators of Compromise (IoCs; e.g., IP addresses) are their participants and traces. Attaching them to these anchors places every per-report graph in one canonical space.
  We realize this insight in BEACON, an LLM-driven framework for cross-source CTI knowledge graph construction. Its first stage extracts each report into a graph under a propose-then-verify paradigm, grounding candidates in report evidence and official ATT&CK definitions, to suppress LLM misclassification and hallucination. Its second stage merges these graphs with a hierarchical alignment strategy that applies signals in decreasing order of determinism, from character-level and semantic similarity to overlapping technique neighborhoods, iterating as merges pool neighborhoods. No existing benchmark links entities to technique anchors or provides cross-source alignment ground truth. We therefore construct and release two human-annotated datasets from 34 sources: to our knowledge the largest for report-level CTI extraction (8,395 elements) and the first for cross-source consolidation (3,487). On them, BEACON outperforms all baselines by at least 23% and 9%, respectively.

</details>


### [32] [LongPIBench: A Long-Context Benchmark for Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28411)
*Yupei Liu,Yuqi Jia,Neil Zhenqiang Gong,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出LongPIBench，用于评估长上下文场景下大语言模型对提示注入攻击的防御能力，发现现有防御在数千至数万Token的真实任务环境中存在显著脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入基准主要采用短上下文，无法充分反映长文档应用中的攻击风险，因而可能高估防御方法的实际效果。论文旨在填补长上下文提示注入评测缺口。

Method: 作者构建LongPIBench，覆盖论文评审、简历筛选、代码审查和邮件摘要四类应用场景。每类场景均包含合成数据集与真实数据集，文本上下文长度从数千Token延伸至数万Token；随后使用该基准测试提示注入攻击及现有防御方案。

Result: 实验表明，在长上下文条件下，即使是简单的启发式提示注入攻击也能取得较高攻击成功率，并且经常绕过当前先进防御方法，说明短上下文评测所得的防御有效性并不可靠。

Conclusion: LongPIBench为长上下文真实应用中的提示注入防御提供了系统化、贴近实际的评测工具。研究结论强调，未来防御设计与评估必须纳入长上下文条件，以更准确衡量模型安全性。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose a serious security risk to large language models in real-world applications. However, existing prompt injection benchmarks primarily focus on short-context inputs, leaving the attacks and defenses in long-context settings largely unexplored. This gap leads to a substantial overestimation of the effectiveness of current defenses. In this paper, we bridge the gap by introducing LongPIBench, a long-context benchmark for prompt injection covering 4 realistic application scenarios: paper peer review, resume screening, code review, and email summary. For each scenario, we construct a synthetic dataset and a real-world dataset, with context lengths ranging from thousands to tens of thousands of tokens. The evaluation results on LongPIBench reveal significant vulnerabilities of prompt injection defenses under long-context settings: even simple heuristic prompt injection attacks achieve high success rates and frequently bypass state-of-the-art defenses. We hope LongPIBench can serve as a practical benchmark for systematically evaluating prompt injection defenses in realistic long-context scenarios.

</details>


### [33] [Exploiting Per-Core Leakage: Electromagnetic Side-Channel Monitoring of Multicore Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28412)
*Daehyeon Bae,Sujin Park,Insup Lee,YoungGiu Jung,Kyeongsik Lee,HeeSeok Kim,Seokhie Hong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文聚焦多核嵌入式系统的电磁侧信道问题，提出按核心粒度识别和监测泄漏的方法，并在商用四核平台上完成原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核处理器已广泛用于嵌入式系统，但其物理侧信道泄漏信号难以获取和分离，导致相关分析与防护研究相对不足，形成安全评估缺口。

Method: 研究揭示多核架构中的电磁泄漏机理，提出首次进行单核心泄漏利用的分析思路；进一步设计非侵入式、按核心粒度工作的侧信道监测方法。作者在集成射频前端的异构SoC平台实现原型，并部署于Raspberry Pi 4B四核嵌入式系统进行评估。

Result: 原型验证表明，可在多核系统中获得并利用与特定核心相关的电磁泄漏，实现按核心粒度的非侵入式监测。实验对象为采用ARM Cortex-A72核心的Raspberry Pi 4B。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多核嵌入式平台物理侧信道分析中缺少核心级观测能力的空白，为后续多核系统的泄漏评估、运行时监测及安全加固提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Multicore processors are increasingly adopted in embedded systems to meet growing performance demands. However, physical side-channel analysis of multicore architectures remains underexplored, as obtaining usable leakage is inherently challenging. Consequently, side-channel security research on such systems has lagged far behind, leaving a critical security gap. To address this gap, we reveal the electromagnetic leakage mechanisms in multicore architectures and, for the first time, demonstrate per-core leakage exploitation, thereby enabling physical side-channel analysis for these systems. As a practical extension, we present a non-intrusive side-channel monitoring method that achieves per-core granularity. To validate its feasibility and practicality, we implement a prototype on a heterogeneous SoC platform with an RF front-end, and evaluate on a commercial off-the-shelf quad-core embedded system, the Raspberry Pi 4B with ARM Cortex-A72 cores.

</details>


### [34] [Quantum-Based Solutions for Security Enhancement in Open Radio Access Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28480)
*Dzung Quoc Ngo,Tharmikka Raveendranathan,Tuan Anh Le,Vinod Sharma,Purav Shah,Huan X. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析量子安全技术在开放无线接入网（O-RAN）中的应用，重点涵盖后量子密码、量子密码、量子认证、量子增强威胁检测及零信任架构，旨在提升面向6G的机密性、认证能力与系统可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN的软件化、解耦化和开放接口扩大了攻击面并引入新的安全风险；同时，具备量子计算能力的攻击者可能削弱传统密码机制，因此需要构建具备量子抗性的长期安全方案。

Method: 文章采用综述与前瞻性分析方法，考察后量子密码、量子密码、量子认证和量子增强威胁检测在零信任架构中的协同应用，并分析其与Near-RT RAN智能控制器、O-Cloud及开放接口的集成方式、部署约束、技术成熟度和采用时间表。

Result: 文章归纳了量子安全机制增强O-RAN机密性、身份认证和信任管理的潜力，明确了其在分布式组件、智能控制器、云平台及开放接口中的应用场景，同时指出技术成熟度、部署复杂性和演进周期仍是实际落地的主要限制。

Conclusion: 量子安全与零信任的融合可为O-RAN构建面向未来的纵深防御体系，但仍需在算法标准化、系统集成、性能开销、互操作性和规模化部署方面开展研究，以支撑安全、弹性且具备长期适应性的6G网络架构。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN) introduce unprecedented flexibility, interoperability, and intelligence into next-generation wireless systems, but their disaggregated and software-defined architecture also expands the attack surface and creates new security vulnerabilities. Conventional cryptographic mechanisms, while effective against classical threats, may become insufficient in the presence of quantum-enabled adversaries. This article presents a comprehensive perspective on quantum security for O-RAN, examining how quantum-resilient mechanisms can enhance confidentiality, authentication, and trust across the RAN ecosystem. It discusses post-quantum cryptography (PQC), quantum cryptography, quantum authentication, and quantum-enhanced threat detection within a zero-trust architecture based on continuous verification, least privilege, and micro-segmentation. Their integration with the Near-Real-Time (Near-RT) RAN Intelligent Controller, O-Cloud, and open interfaces is analyzed, together with practical deployment considerations, technology maturity, and adoption timelines. Finally, open research directions are outlined toward secure, resilient, and future-proof O-RAN architectures for 6G networks.

</details>


### [35] [LLM-Based Agents for Software and Systems Security: Approaches, Applications, and Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28490)
*Jingjing Nie,Jiawei Guo,Krishna Meda,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2023—2026年经同行评审的LLM智能体安全研究进行系统性文献综述，统一考察技术方法、应用任务与评测体系。核心发现是：现有智能体已具备执行多步安全工作流的能力，但在权限边界约束、行为可审计性及可比评测方面仍明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件与系统安全工作通常包含异构证据分析、假设形成、工具调用、结果解释与计划迭代等程序化步骤，LLM智能体正被用于自动化这些流程。然而，“智能体”定义不一致，不同应用的风险差异显著，且评测协议、数据集和指标缺乏统一性。论文旨在建立该领域的系统认知框架，并识别后续研究重点。

Method: 论文采用系统性文献综述方法，覆盖该研究方向兴起以来的2023—2026年同行评审成果。综述从三个维度组织证据：第一，技术路径，包括智能体架构、感知、记忆、推理与规划、行动空间、编排机制及自我改进；第二，应用层面，按所服务的安全任务进行归类；第三，评测层面，分析所采用的数据集、结果指标、轨迹指标、安全保障措施与比较基线。

Result: 综述表明，现有研究已形成可调用工具、维持状态并在多步骤任务中调整策略的智能体方案，覆盖多类安全工作流。但研究仍存在若干共性缺口：智能体概念和系统构成缺少一致分类；任务场景的风险等级与授权范围未被充分区分；评测往往侧重最终任务结果，较少系统衡量过程轨迹、错误恢复、越权行为与安全性；不同研究使用的数据集、指标和基线不统一，限制了横向比较与可复现结论。

Conclusion: 该领域当前的主要矛盾不是智能体“能否行动”，而是其行动能否被严格授权、持续监督和有效审计。未来研究应优先构建清晰的智能体分类与风险分级体系，设计最小权限和可验证的工具调用机制，强化全过程日志、轨迹评估与失效分析，并推动共享数据集、统一指标及强基线评测，以提升安全智能体的可信性与实际部署价值。

Abstract: Software and systems security workflows are typically procedural: analysts inspect heterogeneous artifacts, form hypotheses, invoke tools, interpret outputs, and revise plans. Large language model (LLM)-based agents, which can plan, use tools, retain state, and revise actions across multi-step workflows, are being rapidly adopted to automate this work. Given the consequences of delegating security decisions to autonomous systems, understanding how such agents are built, used, and assessed is crucial. Yet to this date, there remains a lack of systematic understanding of what has been done and how far we are in this field: the term "agent" is applied inconsistently, applications differ sharply in risk, and assessment protocols are often incomparable. To gain a comprehensive and coherent view of this area hence inform relevant future research, this paper provides a systematic literature review of the (1) technical approaches, including agent architecture, perception, memory, reasoning and planning, action space, orchestration, and self-improvement, (2) applications, with respect to the security tasks served, and (3) assessment, including the datasets, outcome and trajectory metrics, safety measures, and baselines considered, over the peer-reviewed literature spanning the emergence of this area (2023--2026). Our synthesis reveals a field that has built agents able to act but not yet agents whose authority is bounded or whose behavior is auditable. In addition to knowledge systematization, we also extend our insights into the limitations of and challenges faced by current approach, application, and assessment designs, which shed light on potentially promising future research directions.

</details>


### [36] [Recognition Without Enforcement: Configuration-Dependent Failures in LLM Agent Instruction Arbitration and External Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28502)
*Jun Wen Leong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，LLM能够识别指令来源和伪造权限，但这种识别并不可靠地转化为安全执行约束；因此，模型自我仲裁不能作为安全边界，必须由外部参考监视器强制执行权限控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理需要在系统提示、用户输入、记忆和工具请求之间进行仲裁，但来源识别、权限判断与最终执行之间可能存在脱节。研究旨在测量这一“识别—执行缺口”的普遍性、稳定性及其对不同模型和提示配置的影响。

Method: 研究通过激活可解码性分析和交叉探针控制，区分来源格式识别与抽象信任表示；在多供应商模型集群上评估权限伪造和记忆冲突攻击，并检验不同策略、提示及部署窗口的泛化性。随后实现结合认证来源路由与能力门控工具执行的外部参考监视器，并进行自适应红队测试。

Result: 模型激活中可线性解码来源格式，模型也能在提示下口头识别伪造权限，但部分配置仍会执行冲突工具调用，说明识别不等于强制执行。多样化新型攻击下的平均执行率为1.21%，但漏洞集中于可复现的模型—提示配置，并可在部署窗口间显著变化，单指纹窗口内最高变化达47个百分点。提示层防御跨模型和自适应攻击的泛化能力不足。外部参考监视器拒绝了所有测试中的伪造、篡改、重放和未签名请求，同时保留合法操作；红队仅发现一个已修复的时钟偏移准入缺陷，未发现密码学绕过。

Conclusion: 模型能够识别权限线索不代表其会可靠执行信任边界。安全代理应将来源认证、请求完整性和工具能力控制置于模型之外，通过确定性的外部机制实施，而不能仅依赖更强的模型识别或提示策略。

Abstract: LLM agents arbitrate among instructions from system prompts, users, memory, and tools, but this arbitration cannot be assumed to enforce trust boundaries. We identify a recognition-enforcement gap: source-format features (role-template position, channel metadata, formatting cues) are linearly decodable from model activations, and models can explicitly identify forged authority when prompted, yet some configurations still produce the conflicting tool call. We use "recognition" in this specific decodable-source-format-plus-verbalized-detection sense; crossed-probe controls show it is not a unified abstract trust representation. The gap is not an immutable property of model weights. Restrictive policies and diverse prompts can eliminate execution on the same models, while permissive configurations and particular prompt-model pairs yield deterministic failures. Across a fleet evaluation (authority spoofing: 46 model endpoints across 6 vendors including open-weight; memory conflict: 48 models), average execution under diverse novel attacks is 1.21% [0.5-2.1%] (model-clustered CI over 14,294 spoofed trials from 29 models), but vulnerability is concentrated in reproducible cells and shifts across deployment windows (up to 47pp within-window per-fingerprint range). Prompt-layer defenses likewise fail to generalize across models and adaptive formulations. We therefore treat model self-arbitration as a capability rather than a security boundary and implement an external reference monitor combining authenticated source routing with capability-gated tool execution. It deterministically rejects all tested forged, tampered, replayed, and unsigned requests while preserving legitimate operations. A separate adaptive red-team found one implementation flaw (a since-patched clock-skew admission), not a cryptographic bypass. Secure agents require external enforcement, not merely better recognition.

</details>


### [37] [Relaxed Sender Anonymity for CBDC Interbank Settlement: A Zero-Knowledge Approach on Permissioned EVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28529)
*Pietro Tiberi,Gabriele Marcelli,Vitangelo Lasorella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向许可型、兼容以太坊的CBDC银行间结算保密协议：发送方在链上保持可识别以满足监管问责，接收方、金额及业务载荷通过密码学手段隐藏，以缓解DLT透明性与商业机密保护之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLT账本的全网可见性有利于无信任验证，但会暴露银行间双边资金流、交易对手和金额等敏感信息。完全匿名又可能削弱合规审计能力，因此需要一种兼顾可追责性、反洗钱/反恐融资要求与交易保密性的折中机制。

Method: 协议采用“宽松发送方匿名性”模型：发起机构公开可见，接收机构、转账金额和业务数据被隐藏。原型部署于Hyperledger Besu与QBFT共识网络，使用BN254曲线上的Groth16零知识证明、Poseidon哈希承诺、增量Merkle树及多接收方ECIES加密。链上NoteRegistry合约以追加式日志保存加密票据，支持充值入池、保密转账和出池三类状态转换，并避免依赖受信任的链下托管服务器。

Result: 在由三家商业银行、中央银行运营方和证券托管机构组成的五节点测试网络中，端到端结算耗时为8—16秒；通过EVM预编译验证零知识证明的开销约为1毫秒、约22万Gas；普通ARM硬件上的客户端证明生成耗时为4—12秒。协议层面实现了接收方保密，但原型的NoteRegistry仍采用按所有者索引的事件，可能产生侧信道暴露。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于零知识证明、承诺与加密票据的许可链协议能够在银行间结算场景中提供可行的交易保密性，同时保留发送方可追责性。其性能基本具备原型验证价值，但生产部署前需将按所有者索引的事件改为统一广播等机制，以进一步降低元数据泄露风险。

Abstract: Central Bank Digital Currency (CBDC) interbank settlement systems operating on Distributed Ledger Technology (DLT) face a fundamental trade-off: blockchain transparency enables trustless verification but exposes commercially sensitive bilateral transaction flows to all network participants. We propose a confidential interbank settlement protocol for permissioned Ethereum-compatible networks that resolves this tension through a relaxed sender anonymity model tailored to regulatory AML/CFT requirements. In this model, the initiating institution remains publicly identifiable on-chain for accountability and compliance, while the receiving institution, transfer amount, and business payload are cryptographically obfuscated. We realize the protocol on Hyperledger Besu using QBFT consensus, combining Groth16 zero-knowledge proofs over BN254, Poseidon hash commitments in an incremental Merkle tree, multi-recipient ECIES payload encryption, and an on-chain NoteRegistry contract that stores encrypted notes as an append-only ledger log, eliminating trusted off-chain custody servers. The protocol supports shield, confidential transfer, and unshield state transitions. Experimental evaluation across a five-node network (three commercial banks, a central bank operator, and a securities depository) demonstrates end-to-end settlement in 8-16 s, proof verification overhead of about 1 ms (around 220k gas) via EVM precompiles, and client proof generation in 4-12 s on commodity ARM hardware. While receiver confidentiality is established at the protocol level, the current proof-of-concept NoteRegistry uses owner-indexed events, a trade-off addressable in production via uniform event broadcasting.

</details>


### [38] [Offline-Verifiable Accountability for Cross-Organization Agent Messaging: A Preserved Evidence-Bundle Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28542)
*Adil Alshammari,Hayretdin Bahsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向跨组织智能体工作流的“保全证据包”与离线验证器，使后续审计者无需依赖在线服务或任一参与方控制的系统，即可按预定义证据规则验证单个事件的证据充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨组织工作流中的委托操作、责任归属与事件真实性可能在事后发生争议；但实时系统可能不可用、由单方控制或缺乏共同信任。现有认证日志、签名检查点和委托机制虽能提供部分能力，却缺少以验证者为中心、可按明确规则离线检查的事件级证据封装。

Method: 作者设计保全证据包模型及规则控制的离线验证器。证据包可包含发送方认证、认证日志承诺、见证者支持的检查点、追加式连续性证明、具委托语义的授权证据，以及在规则要求时由接收方签署的明确回执。验证器仅接受由当前规则规定证据直接支持的主张，不将传输行为或日志包含关系推断为已送达或已接收。原型在300个完整工作流和1200个有效证据包上测试多类规则配置与事件证据要求下的离线验证延迟，并进行损坏或证据不足的负向测试。

Result: 原型结果表明，检查点上下文锚定是当前实现中延迟最高的环节；委托证明和工作流前置条件证明也会增加验证步骤。负向测试中，所有被篡改或不满足规则要求的证据包均被拒绝，未观察到错误接受。

Conclusion: 该方案将审计重点从依赖在线平台与运行时日志，转向可长期保存、可独立验证且受显式规则约束的事件级证据。其主要贡献是明确证据充分性的判定边界，尤其避免将日志记录或传输迹象误作接收事实；但摘要未提供具体延迟数值、吞吐量、证据包存储开销及对不同信任模型的适用边界。

Abstract: Cross-organization agent workflows require preserved evidence that remains independently verifiable during later audit or dispute review. They may involve multiple organizations, delegated actions, policy-relevant events, and disputed accountability claims. This is difficult when live systems are unavailable, controlled by one party, or not trusted by all participants. Existing mechanisms provide useful pieces, including authenticated logging, delegation semantics, signed checkpoints, and consistency checks. What remains missing is a verifier-centered event-level bundle for checking evidence sufficiency offline under an explicit policy. We propose a preserved evidence-bundle model and a policy-controlled offline verifier for agent-to-agent workflow events. Each bundle preserves policy-required evidence, including sender authentication, authenticated log commitment, witness-backed checkpoint evidence, append-only continuity, delegation-aware authorization evidence, and explicit receiver-signed receipt evidence when required. The verifier accepts only claims supported by the selected policy-required evidence, giving a later reviewer an offline basis for assessing evidence sufficiency. It does not infer delivery or receipt from transport behavior or log inclusion alone. In a prototype evaluation over 300 complete workflows and 1200 valid preserved bundles, we measure offline verifier-side latency across policy profiles and workflow-event evidence requirements. Checkpoint-context anchoring has the highest latency in the current prototype, while delegation and workflow-prerequisite evidence require additional verification steps. In targeted negative-evidence tests, all corrupted or policy-insufficient bundles were rejected, with no false acceptance observed. These results support evidence-based audit and dispute review without relying on live services or platform-specific logs.

</details>

### [39] [Time Capsule of Testable Human Knowledge: 41 Years of Jeopardy! in a Single Free Local Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27459)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张：一个仅9GB、14B参数的4位量化开放权重模型，可在完整《Jeopardy!》题库上达到67.0%的答题率，并以此说明通用知识问答能力已从大型专用服务器系统变为可携带、低成本的模型文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者以2011年的Watson为历史参照，关注知识问答系统在计算资源、可部署性和时间泛化能力上的变化。核心问题不是模型是否接触过类似训练材料，而是其能否回答构建或训练截止时间之后出现的新题目。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-14B的4位量化版本，对1984—2025年41季共529,939条线索进行全量评测；采用强制作答协议，并结合精确匹配与模糊匹配判定答案。作者还报告了事实型类别表现，并在训练截止日期之后播出的题目上比较本地模型、Claude Opus 4.8与Watson的可答性。

Result: Qwen2.5-14B在全量题库上的总体正确率为67.0%，在事实型类别中超过85%。对于训练截止后播出的题目，本地模型维持约65%的正确率，Claude Opus 4.8约为95%；作者认为Watson受其固定、人工整理语料的限制，无法回答其构建后产生的题目。

Conclusion: 摘要的主要结论是，现代开放权重语言模型已能以极低存储和部署成本承载广泛的通用知识问答能力，并具备一定的时间外泛化能力。需要注意的是，结论的说服力仍依赖于训练截止日期的可验证性、答案归一化及模糊匹配规则、提示词与拒答处理、类别分布，以及不同系统之间是否采用等价评测条件。

Abstract: In 2011, IBM's Watson was something like a sealed capsule of its era's queryable knowledge. Its DeepQA system defeated the strongest human Jeopardy! champions, but the knowledge that let it do so lived in a curated billion-document corpus running on a cluster of POWER7 servers, frozen at build time and impossible to move or copy. We show that the same kind of artifact, a snapshot of what a culture can answer, is now portable and essentially free. We evaluate a single 9 GB open-weight model (Qwen2.5-14B, 4-bit) against the complete open Jeopardy! clue dataset, 529,939 clues across all 41 broadcast seasons from 1984 to 2025. To our knowledge this is the first time a model has been run over the full corpus. The 41 years mark only how long the questions were collected. What they test is far older and broader: the accumulated body of human general knowledge a culture considers worth knowing, from ancient history and dead languages to science, literature, and geography, with a verified answer for every item. The model answers 67.0% of all clues under a strict forced-response protocol with exact and fuzzy matching, and exceeds 85% on factoid categories. We treat training-data exposure as something both systems share rather than a flaw unique to language models. Watson's case is in fact the more extreme one. Its corpus was assembled to contain Jeopardy answers and it was tuned on past clues, and it could not answer anything outside that curated distribution. The decisive test is whether a model can answer clues that did not exist when it was built. On clues aired after its training cutoff, the local model holds 65% and Claude Opus 4.8 holds 95%, while Watson by construction scores zero. The capability survives the move from a server room to a file you could seal in a time capsule, and unlike Watson it is not frozen to its own moment.

</details>


### [40] [Rating the Raters: Rasch Measurement Theory for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27463)
*Pratik S. Sachdeva,Nathan Boudol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: LLMs now sit on every side of evaluation: as examinees scored on benchmarks, judges of other models' outputs, and raters of human-generated content. Each paradigm can be viewed as a measurement problem, where a latent property of an object is probed with items from an instrument (e.g., benchmark) by raters. Standard evaluation practices often neglect the contributions of each core component to the end result, limiting our understanding of what is being measured. Rasch measurement theory (RMT) is well-suited to this kind of problem. RMT decomposes ordinal ratings into separable facets on a common scale. It further provides a battery of diagnostics that can identify miscalibrated measurements and rater biases. We present a case study of RMT applied to the LLM-as-rater paradigm using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, whose construct was itself built under RMT. We fit a series of many-facet Rasch models to annotations from nine LLMs spanning families and capability levels. Our analyses show that LLMs systematically differ from human raters in severity, item-level calibration, question-order robustness, target-identity sensitivity, and rating scale use, which all would be obscured by standard evaluation practice. Overall, we argue that RMT belongs in the toolkit for evaluating LLM-as-examinee, -judge, and -rater paradigms.

</details>


### [41] [Not All Explanations Are Sought: Information-Seeking Psychology for Human-Centered XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27464)
*Andrea Beretta,Salvatore Rinzivillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将信息寻求心理学纳入以人为中心的可解释人工智能（HCXAI），关注用户何时以及为何主动寻求解释，而不仅是系统是否提供解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释人工智能研究往往强调解释的可获得性，却较少考虑用户是否愿意寻求、理解并使用解释。信息寻求中的认知偏差可能导致过度寻求信息或回避关键信息；对于能够连续执行多步行动的智能体系统，这一问题更加突出。

Method: 文章基于Sharot与Sunstein的信息寻求动机框架，将用户评估解释的预期效用划分为工具性效用、享乐性效用和认知性效用，并分析控制幻觉、自动化偏误、不切实际的乐观、影响偏差、过度自信和确认偏误等认知偏差如何影响解释寻求决策。

Result: 文章提出两类失败模式：过度寻求信息导致注意力碎片化但未改善决策；信息寻求不足则使关键风险和错误理解未被发现。对于智能体人工智能，解释还需支持用户预测连续行动、评估潜在后果并判断何时进行干预。

Conclusion: HCXAI应从“提供解释”转向“促成恰当的信息寻求”，根据用户的实际动机、预期效用和认知偏差设计解释机制，从而提升解释对理解、决策和干预的实际价值。

Abstract: This position paper argues that human-centered explainable AI (HCXAI) should incorporate insights from the psychology of information seeking. Drawing on Sharot and Sunstein's framework of information-seeking motives, we propose that people evaluate whether to engage with explanations based on three types of expected utility: instrumental (will it help me act better?), hedonic (will it make me feel better?), and cognitive (will it improve my understanding?). Each utility is estimated through a lens shaped by well-documented cognitive biases, including illusion of control, automation bias, unrealistic optimism, impact bias, overconfidence, and confirmation bias. These biases can lead to two failure modes: excessive information-seeking that fragments attention without improving decisions, and insufficient information-seeking that leaves critical risks and misunderstandings unexamined. This challenge is particularly acute for agentic AI systems, where explanations must support not just understanding a single output but anticipating cascading actions, assessing risks, and deciding when to intervene. By integrating information-seeking psychology into HCXAI, we advocate for a shift from making explanations available to making them sought: designing systems that account for when and why users actually want to know.

</details>


### [42] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍 Prove2Me：一个面向 Lean 4 数学形式化的开放协作平台，支持人类以自然语言发起任务，并由多个 AI 智能体协同编写、复用和验证形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数学形式化面临较高门槛：参与者既需掌握数学与形式化验证，又需投入大量时间撰写 Lean 证明。随着 AI 编程智能体能力提升，作者希望降低参与成本，并探索由人类与智能体共同参与、结果可由机器核验的大规模协作模式。

Method: 平台将形式化目标组织为“missions”。用户创建任务后，AI 智能体针对目标贡献 Lean 形式化证明。Prove2Me 通过协作机制与专用执行环境，使智能体能够基于已有工作继续推进、共享中间成果并复用既有定理，从而支持多智能体的连续协作与证明积累。

Result: 摘要提出并发布了 Prove2Me 平台及其协作框架，展示其目标能力包括：自然语言驱动的证明任务创建、AI 智能体生成 Lean 证明、机器检查正确性，以及跨任务复用已有形式化结果。摘要未提供具体实验指标、任务完成率或与基线系统的定量比较。

Conclusion: Prove2Me 试图将 Lean 数学形式化从依赖专家的手工活动，转变为可扩展的开放协作流程。其核心价值在于以机器可验证性保障成果正确性，并通过智能体协作和结果复用降低大规模形式化的成本；实际效果仍需结合平台规模、证明质量、协作效率与评测数据进一步验证。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>

*Deborah Dore,Greta Damo,Elena Cabrio,Serena Villata*

Abstract: Fallacies are arguments that employ invalid reasoning, making their automatic detection critical in sensitive contexts such as high-stakes political debates, where public opinion is shaped. Spotting a fallacious argument requires contextual knowledge beyond its pure surface text. This entails world knowledge pertaining to the subject matter under discussion, as well as knowledge of the relationships that exist between arguments within the argumentative discourse. Prior work on fallacy analysis has shown that argumentative discourse structure can beneficially improve classification performance. However, such structure is typically encoded only as static classifier features, limiting its flexibility. Building on this intuition while addressing this limitation, we introduce a guided retrieval-augmented methodology for fallacy detection and classification that leverages argumentative relations of support and attack to dynamically steer the extraction of relevant documents. We evaluate our approach on the ElecDeb60to20 benchmark across 42 retrieval configurations and 14 models, performing retrieval over a 15GB knowledge base of collected political-related documents. Our approach improves macro-F1 up to 0.864 for fallacy detection and up to 0.725 for classification over non-retrieval baselines. These results show that incorporating external knowledge significantly enhances fallacy detection and classification when retrieval is argumentatively guided.

</details>


### [44] [LLM-Augmented Causal Discovery: Probabilistic Fusion of Edge Existence and Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27472)
*Neville K. Kitson,Anthony Constantinou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出概率依赖图（PDG），将贝叶斯网络结构学习（BNSL）的数据驱动结果与大语言模型（LLM）的知识驱动判断表示为边存在性及方向性的不确定性分布，并通过加权融合提升因果图恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖观测数据的BNSL难以可靠确定边方向；LLM虽具备广泛的因果常识，却可能产生不稳定或错误判断。论文旨在利用两者的互补性，改善因果结构学习中的边识别与方向定向。

Method: PDG为每条变量关系边赋予“有向、无向、缺失”状态的概率分布。研究使用FGES、Tabu和PC构成BNSL集成，并结合Gemini、Claude和GPT在多种提示词及随机种子下的输出；随后通过加权平均融合两类概率分布，重点评估简单的50/50权重策略，并在26个基准网络上进行比较。

Result: 50/50融合在26个网络中的22个网络上，其F1分数超过任一单独信息源的较优结果；平均F1提升为0.056，且具有统计显著性（p<0.001）。机制分析显示，BNSL在边骨架恢复上召回率更高（80%对60%），而LLM在已识别关系的方向判断上更准确（96%对77%）。

Conclusion: 将BNSL与LLM统一表示为边存在和边方向的不确定性概率，并进行简单融合，是提升因果图结构准确性的有效实践方案。该框架的关键价值在于利用BNSL的高召回骨架能力和LLM的高定向准确性；后续可进一步研究自适应权重学习、不同数据规模下的鲁棒性及LLM错误知识的校准机制。

Abstract: Bayesian network structure learning (BNSL) from observational data struggles with orientation identifiability, while large language models (LLMs) offer broad but often unreliable causal knowledge. We propose combining these complementary sources through a novel representation, termed Probabilistic Dependency Graphs (PDGs). In a PDG, each edge is associated with a distribution over directed, undirected, and absent states, enabling fusion via weighted averaging. We evaluate this approach on 26 benchmark networks, combining ensembles of three BNSL algorithms (FGES, Tabu, PC) with three LLMs (Gemini, Claude, GPT) across multiple prompts and random seeds. A simple 50/50 fusion improves F1 over the better of either source alone in 22 of 26 networks, with a statistically significant mean improvement of $0.056$ $(p<0.001)$. Analysis reveals that the two sources play complementary roles: BNSL contributes a high-recall edge skeleton (80\% vs 60\% for LLM), while LLM contributes accurate edge orientation (96\% vs 77\% for BNSL). Our results show that representing both sources as probabilistic uncertainty over edge existence and orientation is a practical and effective way to improve causal graph accuracy.

</details>


### [45] [Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine: A Scientific Agent for PDE Discovery with Unknown Spatial Coefficient Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27475)
*YuJie Huang,WenWu He,ZhuoEr Lin,Congcong Liu,Dong Liang,Zhuo-Xu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HER-PDE是一个面向异质介质的科学智能体框架，可从两条不同激励下的含噪轨迹中同时发现PDE结构与非参数、时间不变的空间系数场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质介质中的算子结构与空间系数场相互耦合：系数位置变化会改变微分方程，而过度灵活的系数场可能掩盖结构错误。现有方法需要一种能同时约束结构发现与场估计的机制。

Method: HER-PDE采用“假设—评估—细化”流程。智能体生成完整的表达式树假设，并通过局部候选细化探索结构；假设评估接口仅估计假设中明确声明的系数场，不自动补充缺失项；利用不同激励之间的双向迁移性能评估候选结构，最终在封闭的时间区间上进行审计。实验使用五个二维受控系统和5%的相对高斯状态噪声。

Result: 五个系统中均成功恢复生成算子，包括符号相反但等价的场表示和乘积法则参数化。对九个未知系数场，恢复结果的皮尔逊相关系数中位数约为0.85，相对L2误差中位数约为0.28。

Conclusion: 跨激励验证与智能体引导的假设细化能够有效抑制单轨迹下的结构混淆，使PDE算子和非参数空间系数场得以联合恢复，且无需预先指定系数场的参数化形式。

Abstract: Discovering PDEs in heterogeneous media requires jointly identifying the governing operator and the unknown spatial fields that parameterize it. These tasks are coupled: changing field placement changes the differential law, while a sufficiently flexible field can conceal structural error on a single trajectory. We present Hypothesize, Evaluate, Refine for PDE Discovery (HER-PDE), a scientific-agent framework that discovers compositional PDE structure together with nonparametric, time-invariant coefficient fields. The Agent analyzes two noisy trajectories generated by different excitations, proposes complete expression-tree hypotheses, and combines creative structural exploration with local candidate refinement. Its Hypothesis Evaluation Interface (HEI) estimates only the fields explicitly declared in each hypothesis, never adds missing terms, and scores structures by bidirectional cross-excitation transfer. The selected law is subsequently audited on a sealed temporal interval. Across five controlled two-dimensional systems observed with 5 percent relative Gaussian state noise, the Agent recovers the generating operator in all five cases, including equivalent signed-field and product-rule parameterizations. Across nine unknown coefficient fields, the recovered fields attain a median Pearson correlation of approximately 0.85 and a median relative L2 error of approximately 0.28. These results show that agent-guided hypothesis refinement can recover heterogeneous governing laws without prescribing a parametric form for their spatial coefficients.

</details>


### [46] [Class-Based Heuristic Selection for Solving the Flying Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27476)
*Sanyar Ahmadi,Pedram Asadzadeh,Amanj Khorramian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出类条件启发式A*（CBHA*），针对双列飞行方块谜题中的狭窄空间与空位约束，通过状态分类、自适应启发式和条件化平局打破提升搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用启发式难以利用受限空间规划中的结构约束，导致复杂实例上的搜索性能显著下降。该问题具有代表性的瓶颈几何，可用于研究空位稀缺和目标方块尺寸对移动成本的影响。

Method: CBHA*融合三项机制：用于估计空位稀缺时最小位移成本的通用移动约束；依据空位比例和目标方块几何特征构建的七类互斥状态分类及其可采纳启发式；根据状态类别在深度优先和垂直距离排序之间动态切换的平局打破策略，以缓解相同f值造成的搜索平台。

Result: 在146个基准实例上，CBHA*成功率为93.4%，高于深度优先A*的64%、标准A*的39%和BFS的17%；相较标准A*，节点扩展数减少87.98%，平均有效分支因子约为3。

Conclusion: 实验表明，基于状态类别触发的自适应启发式能够显著提高受限空间规划的求解成功率与搜索效率。该方法为将空间结构约束显式融入启发式搜索提供了原则性方案，但其对更广泛物理约束系统的泛化仍需在其他任务和基准上进一步验证。

Abstract: Heuristic search underlies planning in autonomous systems ranging from warehouse logistics to robotic navigation, yet generic heuristics fail to exploit the structural constraints that govern constrained spatial domains, causing search performance to degrade catastrophically on harder instances. We study this problem through the two-column Flying Block Puzzle, a rigorously NP-complete spatial planning microworld whose bottleneck geometry mirrors clearance-to-size constraints encountered in multi-agent path finding, autonomous vehicle navigation, and block relocation systems. We introduce the Class-Based Heuristic A* (CBHA*) algorithm, which integrates a General Move Constraint to capture minimum displacement costs when vacant units are scarce, a formal kinematic taxonomy partitioning the state space into seven mutually exclusive classes with provably admissible heuristics based on vacancy ratio and goal-piece geometry, and a class-conditional tie-breaking mechanism that dynamically switches between depth-priority and vertical-distance ordering to overcome f-value plateaus. Over 146 benchmark instances, CBHA* achieves a 93.4% success rate against 64% for Depth-Prioritized A*, 39% for Standard A*, and 17% for BFS, while reducing node expansions by 87.98% relative to Standard A* and sustaining an average effective branching factor of approximately 3, demonstrating that class-triggered adaptive heuristics constitute a principled mechanism for efficient spatial planning that generalizes structurally to physical constraint systems.

</details>


### [47] [Benchmarking General Mobile Assistants in Challenging Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27477)
*Yiqi Zhu,Feiyu Gao,Jiaxing Fan,Jiahui Zeng,Minggang Wu,Chenliang Li,Haiyang Xu,Peng Li,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出GMA（General Mobile Assistant）基准，用于评估自主智能体在真实移动端图形界面中的多模态交互与复杂工作流执行能力，并系统研究智能体运行框架设计对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端智能体基准（如AndroidWorld、MobileWorld）虽提供了重要基础，但应用覆盖范围和任务设计尚不足以反映真实手机使用中的多样需求、长流程操作及复杂状态管理问题。因此，需要一个覆盖更多应用场景、具备分层难度且可分析智能体框架设计影响的评测基准。

Method: GMA基于7个开源应用构建评测环境，覆盖生活方式分享、旅行规划等多个应用领域，并设计300个任务，按难度划分为4个层级，从原子操作到复杂多步骤工作流。论文在统一环境、模型设置和任务分类体系下评测8个前沿模型；同时针对智能体运行框架进行受控消融，考察上下文保留、显式状态跟踪等设计选择的作用。

Result: 实验显示，随着任务复杂度提升，各模型性能明显下降，当前移动智能体距离稳定完成真实用户需求仍有较大差距。受控消融表明，合理的运行框架设计能够显著提升表现，尤其对高难度、多步骤工作流更有效；但不同基础模型对具体框架机制的受益程度并不一致。

Conclusion: GMA通过扩展应用覆盖、提升任务复杂度并引入对运行框架设计的系统分析，补充了现有移动端智能体评测体系。论文表明，仅提升基础模型能力不足以解决复杂移动交互问题，面向上下文管理和状态跟踪的智能体框架优化同样是实现可靠移动助手的重要方向。

Abstract: Graphical user interfaces have emerged as an important environment for evaluating autonomous AI agents on multimodal interactive tasks. Existing benchmarks such as AndroidWorld and MobileWorld provide strong foundations for mobile agent evaluation, but their application coverage and task design do not yet fully capture the diversity and complexity of realistic mobile use. We present GMA, a benchmark for evaluating general mobile assistants in challenging real-world scenarios. GMA introduces seven applications based on open-source projects, spanning domains such as lifestyle sharing and travel planning, and 300 tasks across four difficulty tiers, from atomic actions to complex multi-step workflows. We evaluate eight frontier models and find that performance declines substantially as task complexity increases, with current agents remaining far from reliably handling realistic user requirements. We further conduct controlled ablation studies of agent harness choices, including context retention and explicit state tracking, under a shared environment, model setting, and task taxonomy. Results show that appropriate harness design can meaningfully improve performance, particularly on demanding workflows, while the effectiveness of specific designs can vary across foundation models. Overall, GMA complements existing benchmarks by expanding application coverage and task complexity, providing a challenging testbed for evaluating mobile agents and studying how harness design supports reliable execution in complex mobile workflows.

</details>


### [48] [Effectiveness of IoT and Deep Learning for Detection and Severity Assessment of Postelectrotermes militaris in Tea Plantations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27480)
*D. K. C. Senevirathna,A. A. E. Nanayakkara,H. M. C. K. Kulathunga,J. K. D. P. Nadula,R. M. Mapatuna,Malithi Nawarathne,Jaliya L. Wijayaraja,P. D. Senanayake,Samitha Vidhanaarachchi,Kalpani Manathunga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出面向茶园蛀害监测的物联网声学检测框架：通过树干非侵入式采音、CNN频谱分类、风险加权评分与地理空间制图，实现虫害早期识别和严重度排序。模型测试准确率为81.5%，ROC-AUC为0.819，具备现场辅助巡检价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是解决茶园虫害隐蔽性强、人工巡查滞后且难以量化受害程度的问题。将低成本边缘采集设备、声学信号和深度学习结合，可为管理者提供持续监测、空间定位和优先处置依据。

Method: 研究使用连接高灵敏度麦克风的树莓派设备从茶树树干采集音频，并记录坐标。音频经裁剪、重采样和分段后形成2,000个10秒样本，其中健康与受害样本各1,000个；按1,600/200/200划分训练、验证和测试集。将傅里叶变换得到的频谱图输入CNN，输出受害分类及概率。严重度评分综合CNN受害概率、平均声学振幅和5米范围内邻近受害植株数量，并通过地理空间映射展示分布。

Result: 在存在真实环境噪声的茶园现场试验中，CNN在独立测试集上达到81.5%准确率、80.6%精确率、83.0%召回率、81.8% F1值和0.819 ROC-AUC。召回率略高于精确率，表明模型对受害植株的检出能力较好，但仍存在一定误报。严重度模型进一步将单株预测扩展为可排序的空间风险信息。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了物联网声学监测与CNN结合用于茶园虫害早期筛查的可行性，并通过严重度评分和地图输出提升了结果的可操作性。主要局限是总体分类性能尚属中等，且摘要未说明跨季节、跨地块、跨设备泛化及严重度标签的独立验证。后续应开展更大规模外部验证，报告类别混淆矩阵和阈值策略，并比较不同环境噪声条件下的稳健性。

Abstract: Tea plantations are vulnerable to Postelectrotermes militaris, commonly known as the Upcountry Live Wood Termite (ULWT), which can cause substantial damage when infestations remain undetected. This study proposes an IoT-enabled acoustic monitoring framework integrated with deep learning for early detection and severity assessment of ULWT infestations in tea plantations.
  Research Method: Audio signals were captured non-invasively from tea trunks using a high-sensitivity microphone connected to a Raspberry Pi-based IoT device, with geographic coordinates recorded for spatial tracking. After trimming, resampling, and segmentation, 2,000 ten-second samples were obtained, comprising 1,000 healthy and 1,000 infested samples, and divided into 1,600 training, 200 validation, and 200 test samples. The dataset used in this study is publicly available on Kaggle (Senevirathna et al. 2026). Fourier-derived spectrograms trained a CNN for infestation classification and probability estimation. A weighted severity model combined CNN probability, mean acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants within 5 m, with geospatial mapping used to visualize infestation distribution.
  Findings and Values: Field trials in a ULWT-affected tea plantation in Pundaluoya demonstrated feasibility under realistic environmental noise. On the held-out test set, the CNN achieved 81.5% accuracy, 80.6% precision, 83.0% recall, 81.8% F1-score, and 0.819 ROC-AUC. Beyond binary infestation detection, the framework introduced quantitative severity assessment using infestation probability, acoustic amplitude, and nearby infested plants. The resulting severity and geospatial outputs can support plantation managers in identifying high-risk areas, prioritizing field inspections, and implementing more timely and targeted control measures.

</details>


### [49] [Context Localization for Generalized Level-Based Evaluation in Knowledge-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27482)
*Ondrej Hutník,Natália Puškárová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究知识系统中广义层级评估的语境定位问题，证明了“按语境过滤评分”等价于“将可接受语境限制为与给定语境求交”的充要结构条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于事实、规则、案例、标准或证据单元的知识评估中，支持度可能由非加性单调集合函数给出。需要明确何时局部化评分与局部化知识语境保持一致，从而保证语境依赖的证据选择和层级聚合具有稳定性。

Method: 构建广义层级测度：在聚合支持度达到指定层级的所有可接受语境中，最大化单调集合函数。围绕任意语境B，比较评分过滤与可接受语境同B求交两种操作，并从结构上刻画其对所有单调集合函数成立的条件；进一步分析逐点机制、块生成机制及参数化系统中的约简性质。

Result: 主定理表明，一致性对所有单调集合函数成立，当且仅当满足两个条件：语境上的单调性，以及排除B之外存在正局部支持的约简性质。研究还给出了产生该约简性质的逐点和块生成机制，并将结论推广到参数化系统。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了广义层级评估中语境局部化一致性的统一判据，为非加性支持度评估、语境依赖证据筛选和层级知识聚合提供了稳定性分析框架。

Abstract: We study context localization for generalized level-based evaluation in knowledge-based systems. The framework models situations where a structured nonnegative score, defined on facts, rules, cases, criteria or evidence units, is evaluated through conditional aggregation tests on admissible knowledge contexts. The generalized level measure maximizes a monotone set function over all contexts whose aggregated support reaches a prescribed level. We characterize when filtering the score by a context $B$ is equivalent to localizing the admissible contexts by intersection with $B$. The main theorem shows that this consistency holds for all monotone set functions if and only if two structural conditions are satisfied: monotonicity with respect to contexts and a reduction property excluding positive localized support outside $B$. We analyze pointwise and block-generated mechanisms producing the reduction property, extend the result to parameterized systems, and interpret it as a stability criterion for context-dependent evidence selection, non-additive support evaluation and level-based knowledge aggregation.

</details>


### [50] [CareGraph: An Auditable Hybrid AI Framework for Evidence-Grounded Personalized Longitudinal Health Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27484)
*Pratik Ghawate,Tanvi Patil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CareGraph是一种面向个性化医疗的可审计混合人工智能框架，将临床记录、自我报告和可穿戴设备数据整合为可追溯的趋势、上下文缺失提示、有限范围的后续建议及讨论问题，同时避免诊断、治疗选择和自主临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构健康数据通常分散、难以解释且缺乏来源追踪，传统端到端语言模型还可能产生不可验证或不安全的输出。因此，研究旨在构建一个兼顾可解释性、证据溯源、数据充分性判断和安全约束的个性化健康信息处理系统。

Method: 系统采用分阶段混合架构，包括确定性分析、上下文检测、证据图谱构建、受约束语言模型生成、证据验证、安全规则控制和发布门控。研究使用开发集、验证集和留出集进行测试，每组包含400名合成患者；同时开展安全规则基准测试、图谱检索审计，以及与单体式GPT系统在56名匹配患者上的对比。

Result: 在留出数据上，冻结的普通最小二乘趋势规则结合数据充分性门控达到0.827准确率、0.837宏F1，宏F1的95%置信区间为0.819至0.854，不足数据识别F1为0.974。上下文缺失检测严格微平均F1为0.815，高于传统检测器的0.318。安全规则达到1.000精确率、0.950召回率和0.974 F1。80名患者的图谱审计中生成79份、展示78份且未触发回退；1份被阻断，1份因证据键无效而安全失败。与基线相比，CareGraph平均响应更快（40.15秒对49.62秒）、文本更短（661词对1163词），并表现出更好的纵向目标词汇对齐，但基线使用更少令牌且引用更多原始证据。

Conclusion: CareGraph初步证明了可审计、证据约束和安全边界明确的混合人工智能框架在个性化健康信息整理中的可行性。其优势主要体现在上下文识别、安全控制、来源追踪和输出效率；但图谱结构对生成质量的增量影响仍需通过配对实验验证，且合成数据结果尚不能直接替代真实临床环境中的评估。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is transforming personalized healthcare, yet fragmented clinical, self reported, and wearable evidence remains difficult to interpret and trace. We present CareGraph, an auditable hybrid AI framework that converts heterogeneous records into prioritized trends, missing context indicators, bounded next steps, discussion questions, and provenance linked explanations. CareGraph organizes evidence without diagnosing, predicting outcomes, selecting treatment, or making autonomous clinical decisions. Its pipeline covers deterministic analysis, context detection, graph construction, constrained language model synthesis, evidence validation, safety controls, and release gating. Tests used synthetic cohorts of 400 patients each for development, validation, and holdout. On holdout data, a frozen ordinary least squares trend rule with a sufficiency gate achieved 0.827 accuracy, 0.837 macro F1 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.819 to 0.854, and 0.974 insufficient data F1. Missing context detection achieved 0.815 strict micro F1 versus 0.318 for the legacy detector. On an authored holdout benchmark, safety ruleset version 1.2 achieved 1.000 precision, 0.950 recall, and 0.974 F1. An audit requiring graph retrieval across 80 patients yielded 79 syntheses and 78 presentations without fallback; one output was blocked and one failed closed because of an invalid evidence key. Against monolithic GPT 5.6 on 56 matched patients, CareGraph was faster at 40.15 versus 49.62 seconds, shorter at 661 versus 1,163 words, and showed better exploratory lexical alignment with longitudinal targets; the baseline used fewer tokens and cited more raw evidence. Graph auditing verified provenance and deterministic retrieval; incremental graph effects on generation require paired evaluation. CareGraph offers a safety bounded foundation for intelligent personalized health systems.

</details>


### [51] [Thinking Costs Tokens: When More Structure is Worth the Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27506)
*Thomas Nolasque,John Grey,Calista Pham,Ankit Vani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究推理架构的规划、验证与修复开销是否存在预算临界点。结果显示：在低输出令牌预算下，验证式搜索因结构性开销而显著落后于单次调用；预算超过约1,000—1,500个输出等价令牌后，验证式搜索稳定优于单体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型加入搜索、校验和修复能力可能提升复杂推理质量，但这些步骤会占用有限令牌预算。论文旨在量化“推理结构的额外开销”何时由负担转化为收益，并给出可操作的预算阈值。

Method: 作者在FinQA与TAT-QA上比较两种系统：单体系统，即一次LLM调用；验证式搜索系统，即加入规划、标签盲校验与修复流程。两者均使用GPT-5.4 mini，在250至42,000个输出等价令牌的14个预算档位上评测，共运行1,000个案例、完成28,000个系统—案例—预算单元。通过严格的intersection-union检验验证性能交叉点。

Result: 最低两个预算档位中，两种系统均为0%准确率，原因是完整提示无法容纳。在1,000令牌时，单体系统准确率为18%，验证式搜索接近0%，表明规划开销挤占了生成答案的空间。自1,500令牌起，验证式搜索超过单体系统并保持优势；最高预算下，验证式搜索约44%，单体系统约40%。交叉区间位于1,000至1,500令牌之间，且两个端点的严格检验均满足p≤0.001。

Conclusion: 推理结构并非在所有预算下都有效，其收益具有明确的预算依赖性。对于令牌极其受限的任务，应优先采用单体生成以避免流程开销；当预算至少达到约1,500个输出等价令牌时，采用规划、独立校验和修复的验证式搜索更具优势。

Abstract: Adding inference structure to a language model lets it search, verify, and revise, but these actions consume the very budget they are supposed to use well. In this paper, we investigate whether there exists a token-budget threshold, below which the overhead of planning and verification hurts performance and above which it helps. We evaluate two systems on FinQA and TAT-QA financial reasoning tasks, using GPT-5.4 mini across 14 budget tiers ranging from 250 to 42,000 output-equivalent tokens. The first system is a monolith, which is a single LLM call. The second is a verified search architecture that adds planning, label-blind checking, and repair capabilities. We run 1,000 cases for a total of 28,000 completed cells. Both systems score 0% at the two lowest tiers, where neither can fit a complete prompt. At 1,000 tokens, the monolith reaches 18% accuracy while verified search scores near 0%, since the planning overhead leaves no room for an answer. From 1,500 tokens onward, verified search surpasses the monolith and maintains a consistent advantage, reaching approximately 44% at the highest tiers while the monolith reaches approximately 40%. The crossover occurs between 1,000 and 1,500 output-equivalent tokens, confirmed by a strict intersection-union test ($p \le 0.001$ at both endpoints).

</details>


### [52] [WM-R1: Training GUI Agents to Reason and leverage World Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27508)
*Yu Han,Tianwen Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WM-R1提出一种完全依赖世界模型训练移动端GUI智能体的强化学习框架，以降低真实环境交互成本并提升训练稳定性和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI强化学习通常需要大量真实Android环境交互，导致资源开销高、训练不稳定，且难以进行大规模并行采样。现有方法也较少将世界模型同时用于轨迹生成和动作决策推理。

Method: WM-R1在整个强化学习训练循环中使用世界模型生成状态转移，替代真实Android环境；同时将世界模型嵌入智能体的思考过程，使其在执行动作前预测候选动作后果。框架支持基于世界模型的大规模并行、步级轨迹生成，并设计多维规则奖励，联合优化任务成功率、轨迹效率和世界模型利用率。研究还构建了包含2000个高难度任务的数据集。

Result: Android移动端基准实验表明，WM-R1训练的智能体显著优于仅使用GRPO的基线方法和推理时模拟方法。摘要未提供具体数值，但结果显示其在任务完成表现和训练效率方面具有优势。

Conclusion: WM-R1验证了世界模型可作为移动GUI强化学习的主要训练环境，并兼顾动作前瞻推理、低交互成本和高并行训练能力，为减少真实环境依赖、提升GUI智能体训练效率提供了有效方案。

Abstract: GUI agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have showcased strong environment learning capabilities on mobile platforms. However, RL typically demands extensive real-environment interactions, leading to high resource costs and instability, especially in GUI scenarios. To address these, we propose WM-R1, the first reinforcement learning framework that trains mobile GUI agents with world models instead of real environments. Specifically, world models serve as the source of state transitions during all rollouts, replacing the real Android environment within the training loop. WM-R1 also embeds world models directly into the thinking process, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of candidate actions before committing to the final action. Crucially, WM-R1 eliminates the need for real-environment interaction, supports massively parallelized and step-level granularized trajectory generation grounded in world models, and introduces a multi-dimensional rule-based reward that jointly optimizes task success, trajectory efficiency, and world model utilization. For efficient training, we curate a high-quality dataset of 2000 challenging tasks. Experiments on Android mobile benchmarks demonstrate that WM-R1-trained agents significantly outperform GRPO-only baselines and inference-time simulation methods. Code is available at https://github.com/genalyu/WM-R1 .

</details>


### [53] [SETU: An Agentic Ecosystem for Multilingual, Persona-Aware Communication Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27524)
*Jonnalagadda Maruthi Tejas,Uponika Barman Roy,Tilottama Goswami,Samir Goswami,Mousita Dhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SETU，一个面向企业沟通培训的可解释智能体生态系统，用于多语言场景下的销售沟通 coaching；系统融合视频、语音、文本相关性、评分、通知和报告等模块，生成带有模态归因的培训报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沟通评估工具通常只分析文本、音频或视频中的单一模态，且输出往往缺乏可解释性和可审计性，难以支持企业培训与教练反馈。SETU旨在提升多语言、受众适配型沟通训练的可扩展性与透明度。

Method: 系统采用信任感知的智能体编排机制，将视频分析、音频与语音分析、文本相关性分析、综合评分、通知和报告生成等专用智能体进行协同。研究设计了招聘沟通和销售演示两个应用场景，但受评估资源限制，仅报告销售演示场景的结果；最终报告按模态标注分析依据，并保留人工审核的最终决策权。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验数据、基线方法、样本规模或定量指标，仅说明系统能够面向销售演示生成具有模态归因的沟通辅导报告，并支持根据目标受众进行表达适配。

Conclusion: SETU为企业多语言沟通培训提供了一种模块化、可解释且具有人机协同特征的智能体框架。其当前证据主要集中于销售演示场景，招聘沟通场景及系统的实际有效性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Corporate training teams need scalable and explainable tools to improve workforce communication in multilingual settings. Existing systems often score text, audio, or video in isolation, or produce black-box outputs that are difficult to audit for coaching use. This paper presents SETU, an agentic ecosystem for corporate communication coaching aimed at recruiters, frontline sales professionals and training units who prepare for audience specific conversations. SETU is designed for two scoped scenarios: (i) recruiter-candidate eligibility-and-interest calls with persona context and (ii) sales pitches with target-audience adaptation; owing to limited evaluation resources, this paper reports results on scenario (ii) only. The ecosystem decomposes analysis into specialized video, audio-speech, text-relevance, scoring, notification and reporting agents coordinated through trust-aware orchestration. It generates modality-attributed coaching reports for formative training, with human reviewers retaining final judgment. The name SETU (bridge in several Indic languages) reflects the goal of bridging communication gaps across regional languages and audience expectations.

</details>


### [54] [Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator: A Compact Multimodal, Multilingual, and Reasoning Enabled Content Safety Moderator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27548)
*Varun Singh,Anuj Doshi,Makesh Narsimhan Sreedhar,Shaona Ghosh,Katherine Luna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator（Nemotron 3.5 CS）：一个40亿参数的紧凑型视觉语言安全审核模型，可联合审核用户文本提示、图像及助手回复，覆盖12种语言，并支持自定义策略条件下的标签判定与可选简短推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全护栏通常仅覆盖文本或单一模态，难以同时满足多模态输入、跨语言审核、领域自定义策略、低误报率与低延迟部署等需求。论文旨在构建可作为前端审核组件的统一模型，以降低多个专用审核器组合带来的复杂性与成本。

Method: 作者训练了一个40亿参数视觉语言模型，用于输出适合低延迟场景的安全标签；在需要审计或策略复核时，模型还可根据输入的自定义规则生成简洁推理并指出违规类别。配套发布的数据集包含人工标注的真实图像审核样本、良性视觉语言与文档任务、合成的低频风险及越狱样本，以及自定义策略示例，以增强模型的多模态、多语言和策略遵循能力。

Result: 在多模态安全、文本审核、多语言鲁棒性、自定义策略遵循、良性样本误报和延迟等评测中，Nemotron 3.5 CS在引入图像条件审核与策略条件审核能力的同时，整体性能与专用审核模型保持广泛竞争力。摘要未提供具体基准名称、量化指标或与各基线的数值差异。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，紧凑型视觉语言模型能够以较低部署成本承担前线内容审核任务。其核心实践定位是：默认使用快速安全标签满足在线审核需求，仅在审计、争议处理或策略评估场景启用推理输出，从而平衡覆盖范围、可控性、延迟和计算开销。

Abstract: Safety moderation for deployed AI applications is moving beyond text-only prompts: systems increasingly need to judge images, documents, screenshots, and generated responses under policies that vary across domains. Existing guardrails usually cover only part of this setting, making it difficult to combine broad coverage, custom policy control, and low compute cost. We present Nemotron 3.5 Content Safety Moderator, also referred to as Nemotron 3.5 CS in this paper for brevity, a compact 4B vision-language safety moderator that jointly classifies user prompts, images, and assistant responses across 12 languages. Nemotron 3.5 CS returns safety labels for latency-sensitive moderation and can additionally produce concise reasoning traces that apply supplied custom policies and identify violated categories when reasoning is requested. We also release a multimodal and multilingual safety dataset for guard training, spanning human-labeled real-image moderation, benign vision-language and document tasks, synthetic rare-risk and jailbreak cases, and custom-policy examples. Across evaluations spanning multimodal safety, text moderation, multilingual robustness, custom-policy following, benign false positives, and latency, Nemotron 3.5 CS demonstrates a practical coverage tradeoff: it adds image-conditioned and policy-conditioned moderation while remaining broadly competitive with specialized guard models. These results suggest that compact vision-language moderators can serve as deployable front-line safety components, with reasoning used selectively for audit and policy review.

</details>


### [55] [LongGuard: Mechanistic Analysis and Training-Free Mitigation of Long-Context Failure in Safety Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27580)
*Ziyang Chen,Xing Wu,Songlin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongGuard，用于系统评估、解释并缓解大语言模型安全护栏在长上下文中的性能失效。实验表明，随着输入长度增加，15种主流护栏的有害内容召回率平均下降超过50%，主要原因是有害信息被长文本比例性稀释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全护栏主要在短文本上训练和评估，难以反映真实长上下文场景中的安全风险。研究旨在量化长上下文导致的检测退化，揭示其内部机制，并提出无需重新训练的有效缓解方案。

Method: 构建覆盖0.25k至32k长度的Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack任务，并采用Benign-Fill与Needle-Repeat配对设计区分长度效应和信息稀释效应。对6种护栏开展注意力、logit和行为三层机制分析，同时识别护栏专用检索头。提出Chunked Detection（CD）、Attention-Head Sharpening（AHS）两种免训练方法，以及根据上下文长度和审计侧选择配置的Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing（CAHR）部署协议。

Result: 在15种主流护栏上，有害内容召回率随上下文长度单调下降，平均降幅超过50%。机制分析显示，有害信息获得的注意力质量下降，安全与不安全类别之间的logit边际同步压缩，最终导致检测决策失效；该注意力—logit—行为链在控制长度因素后仍保持一致。CAHR结合CD和AHS后，在涵盖合成数据、长上下文攻击及推理模型输出的5个基准上，使6种护栏的平均性能分别提升22%和13%。

Conclusion: 长上下文安全失效的核心并非绝对长度本身，而是有害信息在上下文中的比例性稀释及其引发的注意力、logit边际和决策级联退化。LongGuard提供了可复现的评测与机制分析框架；CAHR-CD和CAHR-AHS表明，无需重新训练即可显著增强长上下文安全检测能力。

Abstract: Safety guardrails serve as the last line of defense against harmful inputs and outputs of large language models (LLMs), yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on short text. We present LongGuard, a framework that evaluates, mechanistically analyzes, and mitigates long-context guardrail failure. We formulate the task as Safety Needle-in-a-Haystack (SafetyNIAH) over a 0.25k-32k length grid; across 15 mainstream guardrails, unsafe recall drops monotonically by more than 50% on average, and a paired Benign-Fill vs. Needle-Repeat design attributes the failure to proportional dilution of the unsafe needle rather than to absolute length. A three-layer attention-logit-behavior analysis on six guardrails locates the mechanism: attention mass on the unsafe needle is diluted, the unsafe-over-safe logit margin is compressed in lockstep, and the detection decision collapses accordingly, with this attention->logit->behavior chain remaining consistent after partialling out length. We further isolate a sparse set of guard-specialized retrieval heads that exhibit partial specificity relative to their base models. Building on the analysis, we propose two training-free mitigations - Chunked Detection (CD) and Attention-Head Sharpening (AHS) - and a deployment protocol, Context-Aware Hyperparameter Routing (CAHR), that selects configurations by context length and audit side. Across five benchmarks spanning synthetic data, long-context attacks, and reasoning-model outputs, CAHR-CD and CAHR-AHS improve the six-guardrail average by 22% and 13%, respectively. Code and data are available online.

</details>


### [56] [Generative AI Expands the Intellectual Reach of Course Based Undergraduate Research Experiences (CUREs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27638)
*Aditi Babar,Kristin J. Davin,Alex Dornburg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究基于连续三个学期的定性数据，考察在生物信息学与基因组学课程型本科生研究体验（CURE）中嵌入生成式人工智能的作用。结果表明，生成式人工智能可通过个性化支架、团队协同支持和促进批判性验证，拓展初学者可开展的研究范围，同时未替代人类的学科判断与智力责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CURE旨在让本科生参与真实科研，但随着研究问题复杂度提升，教师难以持续提供完全个性化、覆盖全流程的支持。生成式人工智能具有按需响应和适应学生需求变化的潜力，但其嵌入CURE后会如何影响学生的探究能力、团队协作及科学推理，尚缺乏明确证据。

Method: 研究采用纵向定性研究设计，在三个学期内收集一门生物信息学与基因组学CURE的过程性资料，分析学生团队在生成式人工智能支持下形成研究问题、开展协作、使用与评估人工智能输出，以及承担研究决策责任的变化。摘要未说明具体样本规模、数据来源、编码框架或因果识别策略。

Result: 研究发现生成式人工智能主要产生三方面作用：第一，按需且个性化的支架支持使学生能够超越教师既有专长，将个人兴趣转化为可研究的问题；所有团队均形成了不同的自我主导项目，而非从教师预设题目中选择。第二，生成式人工智能成为团队分布式认知系统的一部分，帮助具有不同专长的成员沟通、协调和整合工作，但未消除专业分工。第三，能力扩展并未削弱学科判断的重要性；学生逐步学会核验、修改或拒绝人工智能生成的内容，其研究独立性体现在保留对结论和决策的智力责任。

Conclusion: 生成式人工智能可扩大CURE的教学与研究支持边界，使本科生能够探索更广泛的问题、开展更有效的跨专长协作，并承担更高层次的研究责任。其有效应用的关键不在于替代教师或学生判断，而在于将人工智能定位为可被审查、验证和修正的认知支持工具。由于研究基于单一课程情境与定性证据，结论的可推广性及对学习成效的因果影响仍需通过多场景、混合方法或对照研究进一步验证。

Abstract: Course-based undergraduate research experiences (CUREs) broaden access to authentic scientific inquiry through responsive instructor support as research problems become increasingly complex. Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) may extend this support by providing individualized assistance that can adapt as student needs change. However, how embedding GenAI within a CURE to provide support across the research process impacts student inquiry, collaboration, and scientific reasoning remains unresolved. Here we use longitudinal qualitative data collected across three semesters of a bioinformatics and genomics CURE to show that GenAI expanded the intellectual reach of the research experience in three distinct ways. First, personalized, on-demand scaffolding allowed students to move beyond the boundaries of instructor expertise and transform their own interests into researchable inquiry, with all teams developing distinct self-directed projects rather than selecting instructor-provided topics. Second, GenAI became part of the distributed cognitive system of research teams, helping novice researchers communicate and coordinate across differentiated expertise without eliminating specialization. Third, expanded capability did not replace the need for disciplinary judgment. Students increasingly validated, revised, or rejected AI-generated contributions, such that research independence emerged through retained intellectual responsibility. Together, these findings suggest that GenAI can extend the reach of CUREs by expanding what novice researchers can investigate, how they can collaborate, and the level of responsibility they can assume while preserving human judgment central to authentic scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [57] [If Agents Were Angels, No Governance Would Be Necessary: Out-of-Band Policy Enforcement at a Trusted Tool Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27646)
*Marc Millstone,Tyler Akidau,Johannes Brüderl,Marat Pekker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出带外策略执行（OBPE）：将授权、查询收窄、记录/字段过滤与响应脱敏置于智能体推理之外的可信边界中，以降低具有人类凭据的智能体发生越权访问、提示注入诱导和敏感信息泄露的风险。原型实验显示，受保护数据进入智能体上下文、答案泄露精确值或完成禁止操作的失败率从57.6%降至0.2%，但任务完成率有所下降，且该方案不保证完整的信息流非干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有人类凭据的智能体可访问该用户可达的数据与服务，但其自然语言提示约束依赖单一、可能出错的推理过程。恶意或隐藏指令可能影响智能体后续调用，使其超出职责范围、读取秘密信息或执行不应完成的操作。论文认为，仅依赖提示词进行访问控制不够可靠，需要在模型推理链路之外建立可验证的策略边界。

Method: OBPE通过位于智能体之外的HTTP代理实施策略：首先对带类型的操作及资源进行授权；随后在后端请求前缩小查询范围；最后在响应中执行记录级、字段级过滤或值掩码。系统还支持基于参数语义或外部状态的门控，可拒绝或暂缓原本已授权的调用。数据策略所有者定义最大授权上限，智能体策略仅能进一步收窄权限。论文在给定条件下证明策略计划的执行顺序无关，并证明智能体策略无法扩张权限上限。作者发布了采用Cedar类型化策略核心的HTTP代理原型和一致性测试，并在Jira与ServiceNow模拟环境中，对四种模型、含20项自适应对抗任务的基准进行3,621次试验。

Result: 在定义为“受保护数据进入智能体上下文、答案出现精确受保护值、或完成禁止效果”的轨迹失败指标上，OBPE将失败率从57.6%降至0.2%，聚类加权降幅为41.2个百分点，95%置信区间为27.7至54.9个百分点。总体任务履约率由79.1%降至60.9%，反映严格控制带来的可用性代价；但配对的安全且有效完成率提高21.8个百分点，95%置信区间为9.5至35.2个百分点。实验还发现，模型有时可重构从未进入上下文的值，或利用过滤后的行数作为侧信道，因此单次执行的结果塑形并不等同于信息流非干扰。

Conclusion: OBPE将关键访问控制从不可靠的提示解释迁移到外部可信执行层，可显著减少智能体在企业数据与工具环境中的泄露和越权失败，并以“数据所有者设上限、智能体只能收窄”为核心安全模型。其主要局限是安全增强会降低部分任务履约，且字段删除仅覆盖单次执行；掩码和历史规则的安全主张较弱。写入控制、持久化审批、时间维度策略及聚合查询策略尚未纳入评估，未来需要处理重构、计数侧信道和更强的信息流保障问题。

Abstract: Give an agent a human's credential and it inherits the person's reach without the judgment that limits its use. It can sweep every reachable record into model context, where hidden instructions steer its next call, and every request stays credential-valid while the agent exceeds its job or absorbs a secret. Prompts are a brittle guardrail: one fallible reasoner interprets the task and enforces its limits.
  We present Out-of-Band Policy Enforcement (OBPE), a trusted boundary outside agent reasoning. It authorizes the typed operation and resource, narrows the query before the backend call, then filters records and fields or masks values in the response. Semantic gating can deny or hold an authorized call on argument values or external state. A data policy owner sets the maximum grant; agent policy can only narrow it. We prove, under stated conditions, that the policy plan is order-independent and agent policy cannot widen the ceiling. Field removal covers one execution; masking and history rules claim less.
  We release an HTTP proxy prototype simplified from our production system, with conformance tests tying its typed Cedar policy core to the model. Against Jira and ServiceNow mocks, our benchmark compares prompted agents with and without OBPE on four models, including 20 adaptive red-team tasks. A trace failure means protected data entered agent context, an exact value appeared in the answer, or a forbidden effect completed. In 3,621 trials it fell from 57.6% to 0.2%, a cluster-weighted reduction of 41.2 points [95% CI: 27.7, 54.9]; fulfillment fell from 79.1% to 60.9%, while paired safe-useful completion rose 21.8 points [9.5, 35.2]. Some answers reconstructed a value that never entered context or used filtered row counts as an oracle: shaping one execution is not noninterference. Write controls, durable approval, and temporal and aggregate policies lie outside this evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [A Framework for Object-Centric Predictive Monitoring of Collaborative Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27671)
*Daniel Calegari,Andrea Delgado,Leonel Peña,Martín Rubio*
