We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [124] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VERA-8B，一种面向审计推理的端到端系统，旨在基于可核验证据预测执法行动发生前的审计风险，并输出可审阅的审计结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用金融语言模型通常以语言流畅性和一般性推理为核心，可能生成看似合理但证据不足或表述模糊的结论。审计场景要求判断具有充分、可追溯的证据支持，因此需要缩小模型生成与证据依据之间的“落地鸿沟”。

Method: 作者将监督微调（SFT）与群组相对策略优化（GRPO）统一到同一证据标准下，以训练证据驱动的审计推理能力；同时引入拒答机制与不确定性限定，对证据不完整或置信不足的案例延后判断。系统还设计AuditBridge，将原始财务申报文件转换为经验证的结构化记录，并进一步生成供审阅者使用的报告。

Result: 摘要声称，VERA-8B在所评测的全部基线方法上取得更优表现，并能在不确定或证据不足时避免给出无依据结论。AuditBridge使模型输出具备从原始文件、验证记录到审阅报告的可追溯链路。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标、基线构成或数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将审计风险预测、证据约束训练、拒答与不确定性表达，以及审计文档处理流程整合为统一系统。其核心贡献在于强调审计模型不仅应给出预测，还应提供证据充分、可验证且便于人工复核的输出；不过实际有效性仍需结合完整实验设计、证据验证机制和真实审计部署评估。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [125] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PLVR（Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards），将可验证任务的推理过程从模型参数中外置为由确定性与神经原语组成的显式程序，并通过符号反向传播直接从输入—输出示例学习程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调和强化学习将推理能力隐含在模型权重中，难以逐步检查、解释和迁移。对于中间步骤可验证的任务，作者希望通过显式程序表示推理，并利用逐步验证信号提升信用分配效率与可复用性。

Method: PLVR为每个程序层附加带类型的本体，在输出端依据真实答案计算损失，再通过原语签名上的类型推理将所需输入本体向后传播。该过程称为符号反向传播，其信用分配由形式化推导完成，而非强化学习中的估计。搜索过程使用每步契约判定作为稠密奖励，并配套提供符号反向传播库与一致性检查器。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型使用PLVR后，在相同预算下平均超过强化学习方法27.8个百分点，并超过规模大一个数量级的前沿模型13.6个百分点。同一原语库可用于两个基准，新任务的边际成本约为100个程序搜索示例且无需新增微调数据。将损失引导搜索替换为相同预算下的均匀采样后，中位程序得分由65.6降至17.5，表明性能优势主要来自反向传播，而非仅来自类型约束空间。

Conclusion: PLVR表明，将推理表示为可验证、可检查且可迁移的显式程序，可能优于把能力固化在模型权重中的后训练方法。实验支持符号反向传播在程序搜索和信用分配中的关键作用，同时开放的库与检查器有助于将该方法推广到其他原语体系。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [126] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在 Countdown 数学推理任务中引入计算器工具调用，并结合监督微调与在线强化学习。结果表明，工具使用可稳定提升性能，Tool-DAPO 将 pass@1 从 Tool-SFT 的35.8%提升至66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理中常因算术计算和结果校验失误而失败。作者旨在验证：将可靠的外部计算器接入推理过程，并通过训练使模型学会恰当调用和解释工具输出，能否显著提高数学任务的正确率。

Method: 作者先分析错误来源，确认计算错误占较大比例；随后构建包含工具调用格式、调用策略及结果解释的监督微调数据。以此得到 Tool-SFT 策略后，分别采用 RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO 和 DAPO 进行在线强化学习，奖励仅依据可自动验证的最终答案。评测使用新构建的1,024题独立 Countdown 测试集，以避免与训练数据完全重叠。

Result: 计算器工具集成在监督微调和强化学习设置下均带来稳定增益，各项 pass@k 指标约提升10个百分点。不同强化学习方法中，Tool-DAPO 表现最佳，pass@1 达到66.0%，显著高于 Tool-SFT 的35.8%。分析还显示，即使只提供最终答案奖励，强化学习仍能促使模型更有效地调用工具。

Conclusion: 外部计算器能够减少算术与验证错误，而在线强化学习能够提升生成正确推理轨迹及合理使用工具的概率。该研究说明，在可验证数学任务中，将工具增强与结果导向强化学习结合，是提升模型可靠推理能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [127] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出COVER评估方法，通过固定信息边界、下游处理栈和有限团队集合，区分多智能体系统中的真实路由改进与由消息及最终答案变化造成的表面准确率差异。实验显示COVER能识别路由提升空间，但预先冻结的路由器未达到主要成功标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统更换团队时会同时改变消息和最终答案，因此端到端准确率差距无法单独归因于路由策略。论文旨在建立一种可审计、可复现的评估契约，准确衡量团队选择本身带来的收益，并避免将下游处理栈变化误判为路由优势。

Method: COVER预先固定公开信息边界、下游栈G、有限合法团队族及评估结果生成流程，并通过完整覆盖计算有限基准上的精确预言机遗憾。对冻结策略执行其所选不同团队的并集，作为比较任意策略对的最小无假设支持集；同时采用源ID不重叠的数据划分、预先指定控制实验、固定栈执行、跨栈诊断，以及ToolSandbox上的五类团队和14个未见任务变体进行穷举验证。

Result: 在MuSiQue-12上，预先指定的特权正控制将遗憾从0.532降至0.402；后续公开接口控制降至0.424，相比0.554，但属于回顾性结果。在HotpotQA-4上，预先指定的公开直接评分器将遗憾从0.313降至0.110。固定Llama栈中，验证后的路由遗憾改善0.190，但原始答案收益仅0.010，且区间跨越零。ToolSandbox中，声明团队族预言机安全证据完成率为0.768，前瞻冻结路由器为0.637，遗憾0.131，未通过预设的0.10标准；回顾性比较器为0.655，平均使用4.57名工作者，略低于全体工作者的5.00名。跨栈诊断显示绝对分数依赖G，但未发现路由器与最终化器之间可检测的交互。

Conclusion: COVER能够揭示团队选择的潜在改进空间，并将路由效应与下游栈效应分离；然而，当前实验不支持普遍或栈不变的智能体路由优越性主张。其主要贡献是提供一种严格、可审计的测量方法，而非证明某个路由器在所有场景中获胜。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [128] [AcrossVAM1.0: Particle World Modeling for Text-Assisted Robot Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28491)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcrossVAM1.0将机器人视频预测分解为对象中心运动建模与外观重建，利用语义粒子和轻量时空Transformer预测未来帧，在轨迹误差和PSNR/SSIM上优于持久性基线，但语言 grounding 与感知质量仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视频预测同时需要准确推断运动和保持高频外观。传统端到端像素模型容易将两者耦合，并可能被强大的最后帧或持久性基线掩盖真实进展，因此需要一种显式分解运动与外观的轻量方法。

Method: 提出AcrossVAM1.0。首先使用冻结的SAM3-DLP编解码器，将四帧上下文分解为机器人、机械臂、夹爪的语义粒子及背景潜变量；随后通过仅含0.28M参数的时空Transformer对粒子身份进行对齐并推进其状态，并利用冻结的OpenCLIP指令嵌入通过FiLM进行调制。因果双流解码器将粒子渲染的运动信息与仅从最后观测帧编码的外观信息融合，再通过残差细化器和可学习的传递掩码生成五个未来帧。实验还包括oracle、负控制、多随机种子和逐机器人分析。

Result: 在VRS真实机器人轨迹基准上，粒子动力学使轨迹误差较持久性方法降低21.0%。在三种传递掩码随机种子下，未来帧PSNR/SSIM由19.97/0.796提升至20.573/0.8004；原始粒子生成使运动区域PSNR由11.89提升至13.23。但最终模型在LPIPS上尚未超过持久性基线，正确与打乱的语言指令仅使轨迹误差变化2.8%至3.1%。

Conclusion: 显式粒子动力学为机器人视频预测提供了有效的低维运动接口，并能改善轨迹和部分像素指标。当前主要瓶颈是外观传递质量和语言指令的可靠 grounding；未来应加强语言—动作对应关系及高频视觉细节重建。

Abstract: Predicting robot videos requires both precise motion reasoning and preservation of high-frequency appearance, yet monolithic pixel models entangle these objectives and often conceal their progress behind a strong last-frame baseline. We present AcrossVAM1.0, a lightweight, text-assisted video action model that factorizes future prediction into object-centric motion and dense appearance. A frozen SAM3-DLP codec decomposes four context frames into semantic particles for the robot, arm, and gripper, together with a background latent. A 0.28M-parameter spatio-temporal Transformer aligns particle identities, rolls their states forward, and is modulated by a frozen OpenCLIP instruction embedding through FiLM. A causal dual-stream decoder combines particle-rendered motion with appearance encoded exclusively from the last observed frame; a residual refiner and learned delivery mask produce five future frames without access to future appearance. On our VRS benchmark constructed from diverse real-robot trajectories, particle dynamics reduce trajectory error by 21.0\% over persistence. Across three delivery-mask seeds, AcrossVAM1.0 improves future-frame PSNR/SSIM from 19.97/0.796 to 20.573/0.8004, while raw particle generation improves motion-region PSNR from 11.89 to 13.23. The delivered model does not yet beat persistence in LPIPS, and correct-versus- shuffled language changes trajectory error by only 2.8--3.1%. We report these limitations alongside oracle, negative-control, multi-seed, and per-robot analyses. The results show that explicit particle dynamics are a promising low-dimensional interface for robot video prediction, while robust language grounding and appearance delivery remain the principal open challenges.

</details>


### [129] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出通信高效混合专家模型（CE-MoE）：通过减少路由MoE层的数量、以额外的词元混合层和稠密前馈网络维持整体深度，显著降低专家并行训练中的全互连通信开销，并在性能基本持平或更优的条件下减少训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行MoE训练依赖词元分发与聚合的全互连通信（all-to-all collectives），该通信可占据端到端训练时间的较大比例。传统架构通常在每个词元混合层后插入MoE层，导致频繁路由和通信，因此需要在不牺牲模型质量的前提下降低MoE通信频率。

Method: CE-MoE采用异构层布局，将词元混合深度与通道混合深度解耦。不同于逐层交替配置词元混合模块和路由MoE模块的全MoE基线，CE-MoE仅在少数选定层部署路由MoE以集中专家容量，其余深度由额外的词元混合层及稠密FFN层补充。实验在2B至31.5B总参数规模上进行，并匹配总参数量和激活参数量以保证公平比较。

Result: 在2B至31.5B的扩展实验中，CE-MoE相较全MoE基线持续降低训练成本，同时保持相近的验证损失和下游任务表现。在31.5B规模下，CE-MoE减少33.3%的GPU小时，并提升平均下游评测得分和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: 减少并集中路由MoE层是一种有效的MoE系统—模型协同设计策略：它直接减少专家并行中的全互连通信次数，同时可通过稠密层与词元混合层补足网络深度。结果表明，MoE层不必在每个词元混合层后固定插入，异构布局能够在训练效率、推理吞吐与模型质量之间实现更优权衡。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [130] [When Robots Mishear Us: Mapping the Safety Risks of Voice-Controlled Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28518)
*Sihan Jia,Oliver Lemon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究语音识别转写误差对具身智能系统安全性的影响，发现输入转写偏差可能削弱模型的拒答与约束机制，并提高执行不安全任务计划的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能通常依赖语音输入，而现有安全评测多假定文本指令准确无误。该研究旨在填补语音识别误差与具身智能安全鲁棒性之间的评测空白。

Method: 作者模拟多类语音识别误差，并将其注入SafeAgentBench和POEX等现有安全基准中的输入指令；随后比较不同误差类型下模型的拒答表现、计划生成行为及任务执行结果，同时评估自动纠错的缓解效果。

Result: 部分误差会保留原始语义框架，却增加指令歧义；另一些误差会明显削弱模型的拒绝行为，使其生成并执行不符合安全约束的计划。自动纠错在部分场景可降低风险，但效果并不稳定。

Conclusion: 语音识别误差是具身智能部署中的重要安全风险源。未来系统应将含噪语音输入纳入安全评测，并结合鲁棒语义解析、风险识别、执行前验证及可靠纠错机制进行分层防护。

Abstract: We investigate whether automatic speech recognition (ASR) errors in user input can lead to unsafe outputs from Embodied AI (EAI) models. We find that ASR errors can lead to harmful instructions being accepted and executed by EAI models, thereby reducing safety. We simulate ASR errors and combine them with existing safety benchmarks (SafeAgentBench and POEX) to evaluate how different errors affect embodied AI safety. We find that some of them preserve semantic structure but increase harmful ambiguity, while others weaken the model refusal behaviour and allow unsafe plans to be generated and executed. We show that in some cases automatic correction of ASR errors can reduce the risk, but this is not always effective. Overall, we show that ASR errors lead to significant safety risks for embodied AI.

</details>


### [131] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出InstructMesh，一种面向生成式3D模型的交互式后处理工具，可通过区域选择、自然语言指令和滑块控制，对模型进行局部几何修复，以提升其制造可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本或图像生成3D模型通常重视视觉合理性而非几何精度，常出现孔洞、封闭错误、局部厚度不合适等问题，导致模型在后续制造中失效。传统修复流程依赖专业建模技能，使用门槛较高。

Method: 作者先分析当前先进生成工具输出中常见的制造相关失效模式，据此设计InstructMesh。系统支持用户选择待编辑区域，并执行开孔、封孔和局部厚度调整等操作；用户可通过自然语言提示或滑块参数发起编辑。其核心是在中间潜在表示上直接操作，从而实现更稳健的几何修改。随后开展两项用户研究评估可用性与交互偏好。

Result: 用户研究表明，非专业用户能够借助InstructMesh识别生成模型中与制造相关的缺陷，并完成相应修复。研究还发现，用户更偏好将滑块等直接参数控制与自然语言输入结合的混合式交互界面。

Conclusion: InstructMesh为生成式3D模型提供了面向制造的低门槛局部修复方案。通过潜在空间编辑和混合交互，它有望弥合生成结果的视觉质量与实际几何可制造性之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [132] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文论证时空可组合性演算并不要求智能体组件运行在同一进程中，并据此提出跨进程的Logos代理运行框架，以降低故障影响范围并支持会话恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插件式代理将多个能力组件置于单一进程和共享上下文中，导致进程故障会同时中断所有组件及其承载的会话，形成较大的故障域。论文旨在验证该集中式部署是否是形式模型的必然要求，并探索更具隔离性和可恢复性的部署方式。

Method: 论文首先提出四个引理：能力组件不必绑定单一进程；语言模型推理具有无状态性，跨步骤状态可移至模型外部；演算的正确性不变量仅依赖状态空间；因此代理可以跨进程组合。在此基础上构建Logos，一个类似ROS的跨进程代理框架，将插件实现为独立进程，仅通过追加式会话记录共享状态，并在工具调用周期的四个边界注入进程终止故障进行测试。

Result: 在80个会话的实验中，Logos在不同故障边界下均能恢复，且未产生重复副作用。同一故障条件下，单进程基线中的一次故障会中断所有共驻会话，而跨进程部署中故障仅影响发生故障的节点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，时空可组合性演算在形式上支持跨进程代理部署，单进程插件架构并非理论必需。Logos通过进程级隔离和追加式状态记录缩小故障域，并实现了无重复效果的会话恢复，但摘要未说明其性能开销、复杂故障场景下的表现及大规模部署结果。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Grounded Checklist Partial Credit for Agent Skill Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27487)
*Suliu Qin,Lu Yin,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Grounded Checklist Partial Credit（GCPC）评估框架，以人类制定的可复用规则为基础，由大语言模型结合任务指令与官方验证器生成任务级清单，并仅依据执行日志证据进行逐项部分得分判断，从而弥补二元任务成功率无法反映中间进展的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估通常将完整轨迹压缩为官方验证器给出的通过或失败，无法识别虽未最终成功但已取得的部分进展，也难以发现技能模块虽改变执行质量却未改变最终二元结果的影响。人工编写清单成本高，而自动生成清单的可靠性不足，因此需要一种可扩展、可审计且与证据绑定的细粒度评估方法。

Method: GCPC采用“人类治理、LLM实例化”的流程：首先由人类一次性定义可复用评价规则；随后LLM根据具体任务指令和官方验证器构造任务专属检查清单；评审模型仅使用执行日志中的可见证据对每项打分，证据缺失时弃权；最后由独立脚本将官方验证器结果纳入最终得分。作者在去重后的SkillsBench轨迹集上比较GCPC与整体式评审，并通过人工标注验证其与人类进展判断的一致性；同时分析有无技能条件下的配对轨迹，并测试向Terminal-Bench和SWE-bench的迁移能力。

Result: 在SkillsBench的4,455条去重轨迹上，GCPC在区分官方PASS与FAIL结果时优于整体式评审，在共享子集上的AUC为0.689，高于0.619。针对12个任务、96条轨迹的人类评估显示，GCPC与人类对任务进展的判断更一致。在1,946个有技能与无技能的配对样本中，879对样本的二元结果未变化，但其中20.9%的样本GCPC得分提升超过0.10，18.7%的样本下降超过0.10，说明pass@1掩盖了显著的执行质量变化。该流程还可迁移至Terminal-Bench和SWE-bench。

Conclusion: GCPC通过证据约束的任务清单与部分得分机制，提供了比二元成功率更敏感、更具解释性且可扩展的智能体轨迹评估。其核心价值在于量化未体现于最终通过率中的部分进展和退化，并支持对程序化技能效果进行更可靠的细粒度分析。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks in interactive environments, yet their evaluation commonly relies on task-level success rates by reducing an entire execution trajectory to whether the task passes an official verifier. This binary score hides partial progress and is particularly limited for procedural agent skill evaluations, since a skill can alter execution without changing the final outcome. While checklists provide finer-grained evaluation by scoring individual task requirements, costly manual authoring and unreliable automatic generation make trustworthy evaluation difficult to scale. To address these challenges, we introduce Grounded Checklist Partial Credit (GCPC), a human-governed and LLM-instantiated partial-credit evaluation of agent trajectories. Humans define reusable rules once, from which an LLM instantiates a task-specific checklist grounded in the task instruction and official verifier. To keep judgment tied to evidence, a judge scores each item from execution log evidence alone and abstains when evidence is missing. A separate scripted step then applies the official verifier outcome to the score. Across a 4,455-trajectory, deduplicated SkillsBench evaluation population, GCPC better discriminates official PASS and FAIL outcomes than holistic judging on the shared subset (AUC 0.689 vs. 0.619). Human evaluation on 96 trajectories from 12 tasks shows that GCPC aligns more closely with human assessments of progress. Applied to 1,946 matched with/without-skill pairs, GCPC exposes the effects hidden by pass@1: among 879 pairs whose binary outcome does not change, 20.9% improve by more than 0.10 while 18.7% regress by the same margin. The GCPC pipeline also transfers to Terminal-Bench and SWE-bench, demonstrating applicability beyond skill-conditioned evaluation.

</details>


### [134] [Image Augmentation as Test Generation for Deep Learning-Based Image Retrieval Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27502)
*Yehan De Silva,Anirudh Sridhar,Armin Lotfy,Nafiseh Kahani,Yvan Labiche,Ziyu Wang,Frank Ouyang,Clare Carty,Azalia Shamsaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了50种图像增强与生成技术作为基于嵌入的图像检索系统测试生成器的效果，发现天气模拟与SaSPA在保持较好视觉真实性和稳定性的同时，最能提升嵌入不确定性与检索失败率；GAN类方法的真实性相对较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习图像检索系统的可靠性测试具有挑战性，人工标注测试数据成本高。论文旨在识别能够产生多样、真实且有效测试输入的图像增强与生成方法，并为测试集构建提供可操作的选择依据。

Method: 研究首先开展文献综述，归纳50项技术并构建十类分类体系；随后在CIFAR-10、ImageNet-1K及工业合作方数据集上进行大规模实验。实验采用Amazon Titan与OpenCLIP生成图像嵌入，从嵌入空间相似度、由四种估计器衡量的嵌入不确定性、LLaVA评分的语义真实性以及检索失败率四个维度评估各技术。

Result: 在所测试的单一扰动强度下，天气模拟和SaSPA在多个数据集及嵌入模型中表现出最高的嵌入不确定性和检索失败率，并在性能稳定性、视觉真实性与增强有效性之间取得较优平衡。GAN增强方法的真实性评分普遍较低，说明其生成图像存在较明显的合成伪影和感知不一致性。

Conclusion: 天气模拟与SaSPA适合用于构造能够暴露图像检索系统脆弱性的高价值测试样本；GAN类方法不宜作为优先的真实感测试输入来源。结论依赖于具体配置和所选扰动强度，改变扰动程度可能改变技术间的相对表现。论文提出的分类与实证结果可支持基于变形测试的测试集设计，并降低人工标注成本。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of deep learning-based image retrieval systems is a software engineering challenge. This paper presents a dual contribution: (1) a literature review of augmentation and generation techniques which resulted in the identification of 50 techniques which we organized into a ten-category taxonomy, and (2) a large-scale empirical study that evaluates these techniques as test generators for embedding-based image retrieval systems. Augmented images are embedded using Amazon Titan and OpenCLIP, and evaluated across four analytical dimensions: (1) embedding-space similarity, (2) embedding uncertainty measured via four estimators, (3) semantic realism scored by LLaVA, and (4) retrieval failure rate. Experiments are performed on three datasets: CIFAR-10, ImageNet-1K, and a dataset from an industrial partner (March Networks). Across all evaluated datasets and embedding models, and under the single severity level tested for each technique, weather simulation and SaSPA are the image augmentation/generation techniques that produce the highest embedding uncertainty and failure rates while maintaining a favorable balance between performance stability, visual realism, and augmentation effectiveness. The results we discuss are configuration-specific and may shift under milder or stronger perturbation settings. In contrast, GAN-based augmentation techniques are among the lowest in realism, indicating the presence of synthetic artifacts and perceptual inconsistencies that reduce their suitability to produce realistic test inputs. Overall, our findings provide practical guidelines for selecting augmentation techniques that maximize test diversity while preserving realistic image characteristics, thereby enabling the construction of comprehensive and effective test suites for image retrieval systems while reducing the cost of manual data labeling through the use of metamorphic testing.

</details>


### [135] [Predicting LLM Performance from Prompt Linguistic Features: An Empirical Study in Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27621)
*Quim Motger,Alessio Miaschi,Xavier Franch,Mohammad Amin Zadenoori,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，提示词的可测量语言特征能够在模型推理前预测其在需求分类任务中的表现，为低成本、可解释的提示词筛选与优化提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词措辞的细微变化会显著影响大语言模型输出质量。当前提示词选择主要依赖昂贵的模型调用和反复试错，缺乏推理前评估提示词有效性的系统方法。

Method: 研究基于100个初始提示词生成9000个受控变体，改变30项语言学指标，并使用5个开源大语言模型在625条标注需求上进行二分类评估，目标指标包括F1、F2、精确率和召回率。研究采用分层十折交叉验证训练回归预测器，并通过置换检验评估显著性，结合特征重要性分析识别跨模型及模型特异性预测因子。

Result: 语言特征对所有目标指标均具有显著预测能力，R²介于0.38至0.42之间，且q<0.05。句法和形态句法特征贡献了主要预测信号。跨模型的重要特征包括复合依存关系分布、连词密度以及词长和句长，这些特征反映了模型对领域词汇和复杂语言结构的敏感性。词汇多样性未显示为有效的提示词质量指标。

Conclusion: 语言学画像结合标准回归模型能够在昂贵的提示词优化流程之前，提供有效、低成本且可解释的性能先验。部分降低模型性能的语言模式也可能增加人类理解难度，因此提示词设计应重点关注句法复杂度、依存结构和长度等因素，而不应简单追求词汇多样性。

Abstract: Background. LLM outputs are highly sensitive to prompt formulation: small wording changes can substantially affect output quality. This matters in software engineering, where prompts guide requirements analysis, code generation, and artefact synthesis. Poor formulations yield unreliable artefacts, yet practitioners lack principled ways to assess a prompt before inference, making selection depend on costly LLM calls and trial-and-error refinement. Aims. We investigate whether measurable linguistic properties of prompts can predict LLM performance before inference, enabling low-cost prompt selection and refinement, validated on binary requirements classification targeting F1, F2, precision, and recall. Method. We generate 9,000 linguistically controlled prompt variants from 100 initial prompts by varying 30 linguistic metrics, evaluated with five open-source LLMs on 625 annotated requirements. Regression predictors are trained via stratified 10-fold cross-validation with permutation-based significance testing; feature importance analysis identifies cross-LLM and model-specific predictors. Results. Linguistic features significantly predict prompt performance across all targets (R2 in [0.38,0.42], q<0.05). Syntactic and morphosyntactic features drive most predictive signal; cross-LLM predictors include compound dependency distribution, conjunction density, and word/sentence length, reflecting sensitivity to domain vocabulary and complex structures. Conclusions. Results suggest practical implications for prompt engineering, including overlap between linguistic patterns that reduce LLM performance and those that increase human comprehension difficulty, and the irrelevance of lexical variety as a quality dimension. More broadly, linguistic profiling combined with standard regression provides an effective, interpretable, low-cost prior before costly optimisation pipelines.

</details>


### [136] [Operationalizing Regulations into Code: A Model to Enhance Governance and Compliance in LLM Selection for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27703)
*Jonysberg Quintino,Hermano Moura,Filipe Calegário*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向软件工程项目的LLM选型治理与合规模型，将法规要求转化为可执行的淘汰规则和加权评分标准，并通过对抗性场景试点评估其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可提升软件开发效率，但模型选型同时涉及安全、隐私与合规风险。现有法规、标准和风险管理框架通常停留在原则层面，技术团队难以将其直接转化为采购、部署和使用阶段的具体决策条件，因此需要一套可操作的选型治理机制。

Method: 研究采用设计科学研究方法构建三层模型：第一层归纳法规与标准中的规范性要求；第二层将组织治理能力落实为多准则决策矩阵，结合一票否决（K.O.）条件与加权评分；第三层以PAG-LLM评估协议度量生产力与可持续性结果。模型还设置监管反馈闭环，将运行评估结果反馈至规范层，以支持规则迭代。试点评估使用20个基于CWE和OWASP Top 10构造的对抗性场景，对商业云端LLM与本地开源LLM进行比较。

Result: 试点结果显示，商业云端模型与本地开源模型呈现不同的风险画像。尤其是合规淘汰逻辑能够排除部分技术性能较强、但在数据保护、控制能力或法规义务方面存在不可接受风险的候选模型，表明K.O.条件可有效避免仅以性能或成本为中心的选型偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究初步证明，基于法规映射、治理能力评估和结果反馈的LLM选型框架具有可行性。其核心贡献是将抽象合规义务转化为技术决策中的硬性准入条件与可比较指标；但由于评估规模较小且基于模拟场景，仍需在更多组织、真实开发任务和长期运行环境中验证其可靠性与通用性。

Abstract: Integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) into the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC) can improve developer productivity, but it also introduces security, privacy, and compliance risks during model selection. Regulations and frameworks such as the EU AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework (RMF), the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), the Lei Geral de Proteção de Dados (LGPD), and ISO/IEC 42001 establish obligations that are often difficult to translate into operational criteria for technical decision-making. This paper proposes a model to support governance and compliance in LLM selection for software engineering projects. The model is developed through Design Science Research (DSR) and is structured in three layers: (i) regulatory requirements, (ii) organizational governance capabilities, instantiated by a multi-criteria decision matrix with knock-out and weighted scoring criteria, and (iii) productivity and sustainability outcomes, operationalized by the LLM governance assessment protocol (PAG-LLM). A regulatory feedback loop connects operational results back to the normative layer, enabling iterative refinement of the model. A pilot evaluation with 20 adversarial scenarios based on Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) and the OWASP Top 10 suggests distinct risk profiles between commercial cloud-based LLMs and local open-source LLMs. The results provide preliminary evidence that regulatory disqualification logic, particularly K.O. criteria, can prevent the selection of technically competitive models that nonetheless pose unacceptable compliance risks, demonstrating the feasibility of governance-oriented LLM selection in software engineering projects.

</details>


### [137] [Decoupling is a Necessity: Transformation-Agnostic Decompiled Code Recovery under Optimization and Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27889)
*Zhiping Zhou,Xiaohong Li,Ruitao Feng,Yao Zhang,Yuekang Li,Wenbu Feng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReSource是一种面向复杂优化与混淆二进制程序的多阶段大语言模型反编译框架，通过分离词法、语法和语义恢复，提高源代码重建的结构稳定性与语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的反编译工具在生产级二进制环境中容易受到编译器优化和程序混淆影响，出现控制结构崩溃、变量含义模糊及语义幻觉等问题，因此需要具备变换无关性和更强可靠性的源代码恢复方法。

Method: ReSource将二进制到源代码的差异划分为词法、语法和语义三个层次，并采用解耦的多阶段流程：利用语义失真数据库检索经验先验以约束模型推理；使用轻量级预测器重建源代码级控制流骨架；通过上下文词法推断阶段优化变量和标识符命名，从而提升代码可读性。

Result: 在包含超过8万个反编译代码—源代码函数对、覆盖3个优化等级和4种混淆技术的大规模基准上，ReSource取得83%的Top-5源代码检索准确率和0.66的平均相似度。在语义可辨识性方面，其表现优于DeGPT、LLM4Decompile和FidelityGPT，后者更容易出现过拟合或性能下降。

Conclusion: 通过分层建模、经验先验约束、结构骨架重建和上下文词法恢复，ReSource有效缓解了优化与混淆共同作用下的反编译失真，可作为后续软件安全分析的可扩展基础。

Abstract: Reverse engineering is essential for software security analysis and vulnerability detection. Decompilation, the process of lifting binaries to high-level pseudocode, is central to this task. However, production binaries are hostile environments: aggressive compiler optimizations and adversarial obfuscation jointly mangle control structures, obscure variable intents, and disguise high-level program logic. Consequently, existing LLM-based decompilation tools frequently suffer from structural collapse and semantic hallucinations. We present ReSource, the first multi-phase LLM framework designed for transformation-agnostic source recovery. To tackle these intertwined distortions, ReSource conceptualizes the binary-to-source discrepancies into three orthogonal tiers, namely lexical, syntactic, and semantic, and decouples the recovery process accordingly. First, to ground the LLM and prevent logic drift, it retrieves empirical priors from a curated Semantic Distortion Database. Second, to resolve control-flow flattening, it integrates a lightweight predictor to reconstruct the source-level structural skeleton. Finally, a contextual lexical deduction stage refines identifiers to restore human readability. Evaluated on a massive benchmark of over 80,000 decompiled-source function pairs across three optimization levels and four obfuscation techniques, ReSource achieves an 83% Top-5 source retrieval accuracy and an average similarity score of 0.66. By maintaining robust semantic identifiability where state-of-the-art baselines (DeGPT, LLM4Decompile, and FidelityGPT) severely overfit or degrade, ReSource provides a scalable and reliable foundation for downstream security analysis.

</details>


### [138] [Antipatterns in AI-assisted Qualitative Data Analysis: A Catalog of Temptations and Pitfalls for Software Engineering Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27927)
*Rashina Hoda,Carolyn Seaman,Victoria Gomes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向软件工程定性数据分析中人工智能辅助使用的“反模式”目录，旨在帮助研究者和评审者识别会损害分析严谨性与有效性的常见做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能可显著提升定性分析效率，但若缺乏批判性使用，可能导致研究产出加速而质量下降。当前软件工程研究界尚缺少用于系统识别和缓解人工智能辅助定性分析方法风险的战略性指导。

Method: 作者结合长期的软件工程定性研究经验，以及对人工智能辅助定性数据分析发展态势的理解，归纳出一组表面上具有吸引力、实则会削弱研究严谨性和效度的假设与实践，即“反模式”。这些反模式按影响递增程度分为危险驱动因素、操作失误和分析失败三类。

Result: 论文的主要产出是一个分类化反模式目录，为研究者提供识别诱因、规避陷阱的框架，也为同行评审提供描述问题实践和判断其方法缺陷的共同术语与标准。摘要未报告实证验证、案例评估或定量效果。

Conclusion: 该工作强调人工智能应服务于而非替代严谨的定性研究判断。反模式目录可作为软件工程领域负责任地采用人工智能辅助定性分析的初步方法论基础，并推动形成有原则、具有人类主导性的协作式研究实践。

Abstract: AI-assisted qualitative data analysis (QDA) offers unprecedented opportunities to streamline software engineering (SE) research, yet uncritical use risks compromising analytical rigor and flooding the field with accelerated production of low-quality research. While tactical best practices will naturally evolve over time, SE researchers currently lack strategic guidance to identify and mitigate methodological risks when attempting AI-assisted QDA. Based on our decades of qualitative SE research expertise and experience combined with an understanding of the emerging landscape of AI-assisted QDA, this paper presents a catalog of antipatterns in AI-assisted QDA - a set of assumptions and practices that initially appear advantageous but ultimately undermine analytical rigor and validity. The antipatterns are grouped into three categories reflecting escalating impact: Dangerous Drivers, Operational Missteps, and Analytical Failures. As more SE researchers attempt AI-assisted QDA, these antipatterns will help them identify and avoid common temptations and pitfalls, while reviewers can be equipped with the vocabulary and criteria to call out problematic and failed practice. Ultimately, this catalog of antipatterns can serve as a stepping stone in our responsible methodological evolution toward principled and meaningful human-AI collaboration in qualitative research.

</details>


### [139] [CC4M: Code Clone Analysis and Visualization for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28111)
*Gen Kawamata,Yuki Ota,Norihiro Yoshida,Shiyu Yang,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CC4M，一种面向微服务架构的代码克隆分析与可视化工具，用于识别跨服务代码克隆及其潜在维护影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务通常强调服务间松耦合和独立部署，但已有研究发现代码克隆可能跨越服务边界并被共同修改，导致变更传播、服务独立性下降和维护成本增加。现有工具缺乏对微服务结构的感知能力。

Method: CC4M检测代码克隆对，并补充服务边界、共同修改、文件类别及度量指标等信息；随后通过带有显式服务边界的交互式散点图进行可视化，并支持基于指标的筛选，以优先识别潜在维护影响较大的克隆。

Result: 在一个开源微服务应用上的示例表明，CC4M能够揭示跨服务克隆及其共同修改特征，并帮助开发者分析代码变更可能影响的服务和文件范围。

Conclusion: CC4M为微服务场景下的代码克隆分析提供了结构化、可视化的支持，有助于评估跨服务变更传播风险并辅助维护任务优先级排序。

Abstract: Microservice architecture supports software evolution by decomposing a system into small, loosely coupled services that can be deployed independently. Contrary to the expectation of high modularity, prior studies have reported that code clones exist across service boundaries, some of which are co-modified in the same version. Such clones may require changes to be propagated across service boundaries, thereby undermining service independence and increasing maintenance costs. However, existing tools do not support microservice-aware clone analysis. We present CC4M, a microservice-aware clone analysis and visualization tool. CC4M detects and enriches clone pairs with service-boundary, co-modification, file-category, and metric information. The enriched clones are visualized in an interactive scatter plot with explicit service boundaries, supporting metric-based filtering to prioritize clones with potentially higher maintenance impact. Using an open-source microservice application, we illustrate how CC4M helps identify the potential impact scope of code changes. A demo video and the tool are available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0xOIQPFbkUg and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21204195, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [RESTCov: A Tool for Structural Coverage Analysis of REST APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28114)
*Tolgahan Bardakci,Serge Demeyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出RESTCov：一种基于OpenAPI规范与HTTP请求/响应日志的轻量级黑盒REST API结构覆盖率分析工具，无需访问服务端源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统覆盖率分析依赖源代码插桩，难以适用于分布式、第三方维护或仅能通过接口访问的REST API。开发与测试人员因此难以判断测试套件实际覆盖了API规范中的哪些部分及其缺口。

Method: RESTCov将观测到的HTTP请求/响应日志与OpenAPI规范进行匹配，计算路径、操作、参数、媒体类型、响应状态码及状态码类别等维度的覆盖率；同时识别规范与日志之间的不匹配。工具输出机器可读结果，并生成便于人工检查的HTML报告。

Result: 该工具能够在不接触实现代码的条件下，对REST API测试执行情况提供多维结构化覆盖度量，并辅助定位未覆盖接口元素及规范—运行日志不一致问题。摘要未给出具体实验数据、对比基线或性能开销。

Conclusion: RESTCov为REST API测试提供了实用的规范驱动黑盒覆盖率评估方案，尤其适合无法插桩或无法获得源代码的场景。其有效性依赖OpenAPI规范的完整性、日志采集的充分性以及请求响应与规范匹配的准确性。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in modern software systems, but developers and testers often lack visibility into which parts of an API specification are exercised by a test suite. Traditional coverage analysis usually relies on source-code instrumentation, which is impractical for REST APIs that are distributed, externally maintained, and hence accessible only through black-box execution. This paper presents RESTCov, a lightweight tool that computes structural REST API coverage from an OpenAPI specification and observed HTTP request/response logs, reporting coverage across paths, operations, parameters, media types, status codes, and status classes. RESTCov produces both machine-readable results and a human-readable HTML report, helping users inspect coverage gaps, diagnose specification-log mismatches, and evaluate REST API test suites without requiring access to the implementation.
  Screencast: https://youtu.be/mNz2P43OyUc
  Repository: https://github.com/2tolgahan2/RESTCov

</details>


### [141] [Post-Edit Re-Verification in Simulator-Backed Engineering Agents: A Controlled Comparison of Verification-Cadence Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28147)
*Qingchuan Zhu,Shuyue Tong,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究比较了保留与省略“重大修改后应重新请求仿真”指引时，智能体在工程设计编辑后的再验证行为。结果显示，显式节奏指引显著提高再验证率和受限最终成功率，并降低节奏违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程智能体修改设计后，原有工程证据可能不再适用于新状态。作者关注的并非智能体能否自发识别证据过时，而是在验证相关状态和事实固定时，明确的后编辑验证节奏指引是否会改善其验证策略遵循情况。

Method: 采用DWSIM作为仿真后端，以连续阀门压力调整构成8个合成案例。设置两种条件：CG保留“实质修改后请求新仿真”的指令，CO删除该指令；两者均不设置强制门控。评估5个Qwen模型，每个“模型—案例—条件”组合通过实时API重复3次，每个条件共120个评估位点。指标包括再验证、验证节奏违规及受限最终成功。

Result: CG条件下，120次中94次发生再验证，26次出现节奏违规，95次达到受限最终成功；CO条件下对应为32次、87次和35次。由此，CG相较CO表现出更高的再验证率与最终成功率，以及更低的违规率。qwen3.5-35b-a3b表现异常较弱：CG仅1/24次再验证、CO为0/24，且两种条件下均未获得最终成功。

Conclusion: 在该受限实验协议中，明确规定“编辑后重新验证”的交互节奏，与更好的验证策略遵循和任务结果相关。研究支持将验证节奏作为工程智能体与外部仿真器交互协议中的显式组成部分；但结论主要适用于所测模型、合成案例和非强制门控设置，不能直接证明智能体具备自发的证据失效识别能力。

Abstract: Engineering agents that interact with external simulators may need to coordinate design modification with reacquisition of engineering evidence for the modified state. We ask whether first post-edit re-verification changes when explicit verification-cadence guidance is retained versus omitted while verification-relevant state/facts are held constant. Cadence-Guided (CG) retained an instruction to request a new simulation after a substantive modification, whereas Cadence-Omitted (CO) removed that instruction; neither condition used a hard gate. The study therefore measures instruction-conditioned post-edit verification-policy adherence rather than spontaneous recognition that prior evidence has become stale. Using DWSIM as the simulator backend and continuous valve-pressure adjustment, five Alibaba/Qwen models were evaluated on eight synthetic cases; each model-case-condition combination was executed three times via live API calls, yielding 120 evaluation slots per condition. Re-verification was observed in 94/120 CG slots versus 32/120 CO slots; cadence violations occurred in 26/120 versus 87/120; and bounded final success was reached in 95/120 versus 35/120. qwen3.5-35b-a3b showed minimal re-verification (1/24 in CG and 0/24 in CO) and no final success in either condition. Within this bounded protocol, explicit post-edit verification-cadence guidance was associated with more re-verification, fewer cadence violations, and more frequent bounded final success, supporting the treatment of verification cadence as an explicit interaction-protocol component.

</details>


### [142] [From Architecture to Binary: Ensuring Cross-Domain Consistency in Model-Based Airborne Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28156)
*Nils Schlautmann,Viktor Sinitsyn,Benjamin Engelhard,Florian Holzapfel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以仓库为中心的机载软件开发方法，通过跨仓库引用、专用持续集成（CI）流水线和自动一致性校验，降低系统、基于模型的功能设计与嵌入式软件域之间的接口不一致问题，尤其面向资源有限的小型团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在遵循ARP-4754B、DO-178C等标准的开发环境中，问题通常不在于缺少流程定义，而在于不同工程域边界上的流程执行与信息同步不足。现有跨域交付易产生接口漂移、变更遗漏和一致性失效，且传统重型流程对小团队成本较高。

Method: 方法建立在此前以关系型接口数据库为核心的工具链之上，并采用仓库中心化实现：为每个工程域指定主仓库；通过跨仓库引用关联工件；为各类交换工件配置独立CI流水线，用于生成、更新和验证；利用自动接口更新、差异化变更通知及一致性检查传播变更。检查被安排在耗时的代码生成和编译之前，以尽早暴露问题并减少人工同步工作。

Result: 论文归纳了跨域协作中的重复性失效模式，并给出相应的仓库与CI机制。该方案已在一个持续开展的实验项目中完成初步实现；早期定性反馈表明，其能够以较低流程负担改善变更传播和接口一致性发现效率。摘要未提供量化指标、对照实验或完整的缺陷减少数据。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是将跨域一致性保障从依赖人工协调和重型过程，转向由仓库、工件引用与自动化CI验证共同驱动的轻量级机制。其适用价值主要在于小型机载软件团队，但实际效果仍需通过更大规模项目、量化评估以及对认证证据完整性的验证进一步确认。

Abstract: This paper presents an airborne software development approach for manned and unmanned aerial vehicles aimed at reducing inconsistencies across system, model-based functional, and embedded software domains. In environments influenced by standards such as ARP-4754B and DO-178C, these inconsistencies typically stem from insufficient enforcement across domain boundaries rather than missing process definitions. Building on a previously proposed toolchain centered on a relational interface database, we identify recurring failure modes and propose a repository-centered implementation to address them, tailored to small, resource-constrained teams operating without heavyweight process overhead. Each domain is assigned a primary repository with cross-repository references and dedicated CI pipelines that generate, update, and validate the exchanged artifacts. Automated interface updates, differential change notifications, and consistency checks propagate changes with minimal manual effort and surface inconsistencies before the time-consuming code-generation and compilation steps. An initial implementation in an ongoing experimental project is described, with qualitative feedback from its early use.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptive Strategy Generation for Boundary Value Exploration Beyond Numeric Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28230)
*Sabinakhon Akbarova,Felix Dobslaw,Robert Feldt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ABEX 是一种面向黑盒边界值探索的智能体式大语言模型框架。它以自然语言策略生成替代人工设计变异算子，扩展了自动化边界探索对字符串、数组及混合输入的支持，并在边界覆盖质量和故障揭示能力上优于质量多样性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件行为常在输入区域边界处发生突变，而这些位置通常更易暴露缺陷。现有自动化边界值探索方法依赖针对输入类型乃至具体函数人工设计的变异算子，因此主要适用于数值输入，难以泛化到非数值和复杂混合输入。论文旨在降低算子工程成本，并提升边界探索的适用范围、适应性与测试有效性。

Method: 论文提出 ABEX 框架，由专门化的大语言模型智能体负责提出、筛选和执行边界探索策略。策略以自然语言表示，可同时包含输入类型通用知识与被测函数特定知识；系统根据程序执行反馈和质量多样性归档持续调整策略，并可保存、复用有效策略。实验采用黑盒设定，在20个涵盖数值、字符串、数组和混合输入的函数上评估；同时通过与质量多样性方法对比、变异测试及消融实验验证效果。

Result: 在11个数值函数中，ABEX 在其中10个函数上的表现优于最先进的质量多样性基线，平均 QD 分数高出11.7倍。对于此前自动化黑盒边界值探索未覆盖的非数值函数，ABEX 在全部实验对象中均发现了与领域语义一致的边界行为。使用相同规模测试集时，ABEX 的平均变异得分为86.2%，高于基线的61.9%，并能检测到约9倍数量的顽固变异体。消融实验表明，自适应策略生成是性能提升的主要来源。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，大语言模型智能体能够通过执行反馈驱动的自然语言策略生成，有效替代传统边界值探索中的人工变异算子设计。ABEX 不仅显著提升数值输入场景中的探索质量和故障揭示能力，还将黑盒自动化边界探索扩展到字符串、数组及混合输入。其核心贡献在于策略的自适应生成、可复用性以及对函数特定语义的建模能力；但摘要未说明策略生成的成本、模型稳定性及更大规模真实软件上的泛化情况，这些仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Software behavior often changes abruptly at boundaries between input regions, and these transitions are known to be fault-prone. Boundary Value Exploration (BVE) automates boundary discovery by searching for pairs of similar inputs that nevertheless trigger different program behaviors. Existing automated BVE techniques rely on mutation operators hand-engineered for each input type, or even for each function under test, which has confined their use to numeric inputs. We present ABEX, an agentic LLM-based framework that replaces operator engineering with adaptive strategy generation: specialized LLM agents propose, select, and execute boundary-exploration strategies, guided by execution feedback and a quality-diversity (QD) archive. Because strategies are expressed in natural language, they can encode both type-level and function-specific knowledge, and effective strategies can even be stored and reused. We evaluate ABEX in a black-box setting on 20 functions with numeric, string, array, and mixed inputs. On numeric functions, ABEX outperforms a state-of-the-art QD method on 10 of 11 functions, with average QD-scores 11.7x higher. On non-numeric functions, addressed here for the first time in automated black-box BVE, ABEX discovers domain-aligned boundary behaviors for all subjects. Mutation testing shows the discovered boundaries are fault-revealing: with equally sized test suites, ABEX reaches an average mutation score of 86.2% versus 61.9% for the QD baseline, and kills nine times as many hard-to-detect stubborn mutants. An ablation study identifies adaptive strategy generation as the primary driver of these gains.

</details>


### [144] [Where Does Balance Break? Boundary Discovery for Game Balance Testing under a Finite Simulation Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28364)
*Hiroki Mukai,Yusaku Kato,Norihiro Yoshida,Erina Makihara,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将非确定性游戏中的平衡回归测试建模为有限仿真预算下的边界发现问题，并提出BBExplorer以高效定位平衡与失衡区域之间的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统常具有非确定性执行、高维行为空间和不稳定的单次判定结果，导致穷举测试成本高且难以准确界定可接受行为范围。游戏平衡测试尤其需要在重复模拟和复杂参数空间中识别由微小参数变化引发的失衡区域。

Method: BBExplorer结合多方向候选生成、预算感知的两阶段筛选以及自适应步长收缩机制。该方法在有限仿真预算下优先探索潜在边界区域，并逐步缩小搜索步长以提高边界定位精度。

Result: 在两个复杂度不同的游戏上的实验表明，BBExplorer在低维参数空间中表现较强，在高维空间中仍保持有效性；同时，在未见过的随机种子和不同阈值设置下，其发现的边界具有较好的稳定性。

Conclusion: BBExplorer能够有效支持非确定性、预算受限环境中的游戏平衡回归测试，并为更广泛的边界导向软件测试提供了可行方法。

Abstract: Software testing often relies on assumptions such as reproducible executions and stable correctness criteria. However, many modern software systems exhibit non-deterministic executions and large behavior spaces, making exhaustive exploration impractical and single-run judgments unreliable. These characteristics make it difficult to identify where acceptable behavior ends and problematic behavior begins. Competitive multiplayer games represent a challenging instance of such systems, where balance must be maintained so that no single strategy dominates. Even small parameter changes can trigger abrupt balance disruption, yet detecting such failures requires repeated simulations under non-deterministic outcomes and high-dimensional parameter spaces. In this paper, we formulate game balance regression testing as a boundary-discovery problem under a finite simulation budget. The objective is to efficiently identify inputs near the boundary that separates balanced and unbalanced regions. To address this problem, we propose BBExplorer, which combines multi-directional candidate generation, budget-aware two-stage screening, and adaptive step-size shrinkage for boundary refinement. Experimental results on two games with different levels of complexity show that the approach is strong in low-dimensional settings and remains effective in higher-dimensional ones. It also exhibits stable boundary behavior across unseen random seeds and threshold settings. These results indicate that BBExplorer is effective for practical balance regression testing and, more broadly, for boundary-oriented testing in non-deterministic, budget-constrained systems.

</details>


### [145] [Sustainability of Open-Source Machine Learning Robustness Assessment Tools: A Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28396)
*Joshua Owotogbe,Indika Kumara,Willem-Jan van den Heuvel,Damian Tamburri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源机器学习鲁棒性工具生态开展了实证研究，发现社区参与度、维护活动和项目持续性分布不均，长期活跃主要集中在少数项目中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性评估工具被用于应对对抗扰动、分布偏移和其他运行压力，但现有研究较少关注这些工具的维护状况、社区参与和长期可持续性。这些因素会影响工具选择、评估复现和人工智能保障证据的可靠性。

Method: 基于既有研究构建种子集合，系统检索GitHub并确定28个鲁棒性工具仓库；随后利用成熟的软件工程指标分析仓库工件，以评估社区参与度、维护活动和项目生命周期。

Result: 研究表明，参与度和维护活动高度不均衡，持续活跃主要集中于少数仓库。截至2026年1月21日，28个仓库中有5个被归类为活跃、22个为不活跃、1个已归档。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性工具不应被视为静态依赖，而应被视为持续演化的软件系统。使用者需要关注其维护状态、社区活跃度和长期可持续性，以提升鲁棒性评估的可靠性与可复现性。

Abstract: Robustness evaluation is essential for deploying machine-learning (ML) systems in real-world settings, where models may face adversarial perturbations, distribution shifts, and other operational stressors. Many open-source tools, including Adversarial Robustness Toolbox, Foolbox, and Robustness Gym, support robustness testing and evaluation. However, little is known about how these tools are maintained, publicly engaged with, and sustained over time, even though practitioners may rely on them to select evaluation dependencies, reproduce robustness assessments, and provide evidence for AI assurance. We present an empirical study of the open-source robustness tooling ecosystem. Starting from a curated seed set derived from prior work, we systematically searched GitHub and identified 28 robustness-tool repositories. We analyzed repository artifacts to characterize observable community engagement, maintenance activity, and project longevity using established software-engineering metrics. Our results show that engagement and maintenance are unevenly distributed, with sustained activity concentrated in a small subset of repositories. At the data collection date of January 21, 2026, five repositories were classified as active, 22 as inactive, and one as archived. These findings highlight the need to treat robustness tools as evolving software systems.

</details>


### [146] [Recovering Software Architecture Intent from Historical Work Items using Generative AI: A Mixed-Methods Industry Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28403)
*Dominik Storck,Tobias Eisenreich,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一套基于大语言模型的半自动化流程，从历史敏捷工作项中恢复C4架构图，并在两个工业项目中验证其可用性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构的设计意图常未完整体现在代码中，而是分散于持续演化的工作项、用户故事和跟踪文档。现有敏捷工件难以直接用于架构分析，因此需要将这些非结构化过程数据转化为可视化架构基线。

Method: 研究设计了五步提示链工作流，将Azure DevOps工作项转换为C4图相关实体与关系；流程结合双向可追溯性和链式推理，以保留工件来源并增强抽取过程。评估采用混合方法：对两个工业项目开展从业者访谈，并对多次生成结果进行定量稳定性分析。

Result: 受访实践者认为生成的架构基线具有较高准确性，对系统理解很有帮助。由于结果严格受输入工件约束，生成图反映的是已记录的设计意图；将其与实际实现对照时，可暴露文档缺口、意图偏差和架构漂移。定量结果显示，架构实体的生成稳定性较高，但实体关系的稳定性较低，且方差会在多步生成过程中累积。

Conclusion: 基于开发过程工件的大语言模型辅助架构恢复具有实践可行性，尤其适合建立设计意图层面的架构基线与支持架构演化分析。但关系抽取仍是主要可靠性瓶颈，后续工作应通过结构化约束、校验机制或人工复核降低多步骤误差传播。

Abstract: Software architecture is often only partially captured in code, while much of the design intent lives in evolving project artifacts. In agile projects, work items, user stories, and related tracking documents preserve valuable traces of that intent, but they rarely support direct architectural analysis. This work investigates the recovery of C4 architecture diagrams from historical agile work items using an LLM-based pipeline. The semi-automatic five-step workflow employs a prompt chain, bidirectional traceability, and Chain-of-Thought reasoning to transform unstructured Azure DevOps work items into visual artifacts. Evaluated on two industry projects, we use a mixed-methods design combining qualitative expert interviews with a quantitative stability analysis. Practitioners perceive the generated architectural baselines as accurate and highly useful for system comprehension. Strictly bound by their input data, the artifacts mirror the documented intent, thereby surfacing discrepancies and architectural drift when compared to the implemented reality. Quantitatively, the workflow exhibits high stability for architectural entities but lower stability for their relationships, with relative variance compounding across generation steps. The proposed workflow demonstrates the practical viability of LLM-assisted architectural recovery based on development process artifacts.

</details>


### [147] [On the Maintenance and Co-evolution of Agent Plugins: An Empirical Study of Claude Code Plugin Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28497)
*Ahmed Hereiz,Yingzhe Lyu,Hao Li,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于1,926个代码智能体插件市场仓库、8,351个插件和77,773次提交，发现该生态快速扩张、以功能迭代为主，并呈现由自然语言指令与脚本协同演化所驱动的新型维护依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体插件通过指令文件、脚本和配置共同提供能力，其维护模式可能不同于以源代码为核心的传统软件包。研究旨在检验这些插件究竟是一次性产物，还是需要跨组件持续维护与协同演化的工程制品。

Method: 作者对插件市场仓库进行大规模实证分析，统计市场增长、插件任务类别、提交类型、智能体参与提交比例及组件共变更关系；并将提交分布与传统开源软件进行比较，进一步评估技能目录中指令文件与实现脚本的功能耦合程度。

Result: 市场启动后六个月内，涉及插件的提交活动增长8.8倍；面向软件工程任务的插件占61.3%。功能类提交占39.6%，高于传统开源软件的17.2%；34.9%的提交由Claude共同署名。文档、性能、风格和重构四类提交在该生态中的语义与传统软件不同。多数组件独立演化，但技能目录中的自然语言指令与脚本存在显著共演化，78%的共同变更具有功能耦合。

Conclusion: 代码智能体插件并非普遍一次性开发产物，而是具有持续维护需求的复合软件制品。其关键维护特征是指令与实现之间的功能性依赖，因此插件市场的质量保障、版本管理和维护工具应将自然语言工件视为与代码同等重要的演化对象。

Abstract: AI coding agents, software tools that automate development tasks through reasoning and tool use, are increasingly extended through plugin marketplaces, yet the structure, maintenance, and co-evolution dynamics of these emerging repositories remain empirically unexplored. Unlike traditional software packages that deliver functionality through source code, agent plugins deliver functionality through a combination of natural-language instruction files, scripts, and configuration files, raising the question of whether these plugins are maintained artifacts that co-evolve across components, or one-off artifacts that developers write once and do not need to revisit. To study the maintenance and co-evolution of agent plugins, we conduct an empirical study of 1,926 repositories hosting Claude Code plugin marketplaces, analyzing 8,351 plugins and 77,773 commits across 2,018 marketplaces. We find that the marketplace is expanding rapidly, plugin-touching commit activity growing 8.8x over six months after the October 2025 launch, and plugins targeting Software Engineering tasks accounting for 61.3% of all plugins. Plugin development is predominantly feature-driven, with feature commits occurring at more than twice the rate of conventional open-source software (OSS) (39.6% vs. 17.2%). Claude co-authors 34.9% of all commits, and four commit types (docs, perf, style, and refactor) carry substantially different meanings in plugin repositories than in traditional software. Most component types evolve independently, but within skills directories, natural-language instruction files and implementation scripts co-evolve at above-chance rates, with 78% of co-changes being functionally coupled, representing a new class of maintenance dependency not observed in traditional software engineering.

</details>


### [148] [A System-of-Systems Case Study for the Verification of Composed Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28498)
*Mennatullah T. Khedr,Mengwei Xu,John S. Fitzgerald,Peter Gorm Larsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究聚焦系统之系统（SoS）中数字孪生（DT）的验证与确认问题，提出将相关性、可验证性、可替代性和保真度等质量属性转化为可验证性质，并通过VDM-RT温室案例分析组合验证面临的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DT工程方法缺少可操作的质量属性形式化与验证指导；在SoS场景中，多个DT的组合使单体质量保证如何支撑整体可信性成为关键问题。

Method: 研究构建了一个DT赋能温室SoS的VDM-RT可执行形式模型；以性质集合方式刻画DT质量属性；分析不同架构角色下质量属性含义的变化，并识别可用于验证这些性质的形式化建模、分析技术与工具。

Result: 案例表明，DT质量不能以统一方式直接适用于所有组件，其解释需随DT在架构中的角色调整。单个DT获得的局部质量保证可作为SoS组合推理中的假设，但这些假设在系统级组合时仍会遭遇显著的形式化与验证障碍。

Conclusion: 将DT质量操作化为可验证性质是支持可信DT工程的可行路径，VDM-RT等形式化方法能够支撑单体DT建模与分析。完整的SoS级DT组合形式化及端到端验证尚未解决，是后续研究重点。

Abstract: Current approaches to engineering dependable Digital Twins (DTs) of Cyber-Physical Systems lack practical guidance on how qualities such as relevance, verifiability, substitutability and fidelity may be formalised and verified. This need is amplified in Systems-of-Systems (SoS), where reliance is placed on the composition of DTs. The goal of this study is to identify foundational challenges that a framework for DT validation and verification should address in an SoS setting, and to investigate the formalisation of individual DT artefacts as a step towards compositional reasoning. We present a case study based on a DT-enabled greenhouse SoS modelled in VDM-RT (Vienna Development Method, Real-Time), including executable formal models, a property-based account of DT qualities, and an analysis of the obstacles arising when attempting to compose these artefacts at the SoS level. We consider how DT qualities may be operationalised as sets of verifiable properties. Formal modelling and analysis techniques and tools supporting their verification are identified. The study reveals how interpretations of DT qualities must adapt to different architectural roles and how local quality guarantees form assumptions for compositional reasoning. Full formalisation and verification of SoS composition is identified as future work.

</details>


### [149] [Rethinking Vulnerability Remediation as a Capacity Allocation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28509)
*Jana Stucke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将漏洞修复视为流量控制与容量分配问题，而非单纯的漏洞排序问题。结果表明，许多漏洞队列处于或超过处理容量，队列状态、处理顺序和容量配置对修复时延的影响大于复杂的优先级预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能提升漏洞发现速度，修复吞吐量可能成为主要瓶颈。研究旨在评估现有漏洞队列的负载状态、预测修复时延的有效性，以及不同流量控制和容量分配策略的实际影响。

Method: 研究使用Apache Jira、Mozilla Bugzilla、Red Hat安全勘误、五个公开Jira组织以及npm依赖图数据。分析漏洞解决时间分布、到达率与队列容量的关系，比较基于队列上下文的预测模型与项目级基线，并评估严重性排序、队列排空状态、容量预留及负责人级容量与专业连接的影响。

Result: Apache漏洞解决时间呈显著重尾分布；主要问题跟踪系统中约94%至100%的到达请求进入估计已达到或超过容量的队列。队列上下文模型的预测区分度仅为中等，且大体不优于简单的项目级基线。不同系统中严重性对修复速度的区分能力差异明显。由过载转为排空的队列对应更短的解决时间；在容量固定时优先处理高严重性漏洞可减少关键漏洞延迟，容量预留可降低关键漏洞长期滞留风险。负责人层面显示，闲置容量只有在需求发生的位置可用，或能够通过相关专业连接转移时才真正有效。

Conclusion: 漏洞修复应采用流量控制和容量配置视角，重点管理队列负载、修复顺序、关键漏洞容量预留及跨负责人专业能力调度。单纯提高优先级排序准确率不足以解决修复吞吐量瓶颈。

Abstract: As AI accelerates vulnerability discovery, remediation throughput may become a greater constraint than prioritisation accuracy. This study evaluates vulnerability remediation as a flow-control problem using Apache Jira, Mozilla Bugzilla, Red Hat security errata, five public Jira organisations, and an npm dependency graph. Apache resolution times are strongly heavy-tailed, while 94-100% of arrivals in the primary issue trackers enter queues estimated to be at or above capacity. Queue-context models provide only moderate predictive discrimination and are largely matched by simple project-level baselines. Severity-to-speed discrimination varies substantially across systems. Flow-control analyses show larger operational effects: transitions from overloaded to draining queues are associated with shorter resolution times, severity-first sequencing reduces critical-item delay at fixed capacity, and capacity reservation can reduce prolonged critical-item delays. Owner-level analyses further show that available capacity is useful only when it is located where demand occurs or can be transferred through relevant expertise connections. These findings support treating vulnerability remediation as a flow-control and capacity-allocation problem rather than solely a ranking problem.

</details>
